- **Substantive design decisions documented in code:**
  - **Plan-then-apply pattern faithful to PR #42 (recon) precedent.** The planner is pure: same inputs → same `CalibrationPlan`; no I/O; no audit writes; no DB reads. The dispatcher (Week 4) owns: (1) the audit write via `services.audit.writer.append_audit_event(plan.audit_event.event_type, plan.audit_event.payload)` capturing the `audit_event_uuid`; (2) the row INSERT into `slippage_calibration_versions` with the captured UUID + the planner-supplied `new_version_id` + `version_no` + JSONB-serialized `per_market_coefficients` + `data_window_*` + `calibrated_at_utc` + trigger.value; (3) conditionally on `becomes_head`, the HEAD-pointer swap inside ONE transaction (`UPDATE ... SET is_head = FALSE WHERE is_head` then `UPDATE ... SET is_head = TRUE WHERE id = :new_version_id`; the partial unique index `slippage_head` makes a partial swap raise rather than leave dual-HEAD state). This isolation — planner returns a struct as data; caller is the I/O boundary — is the standard for everything in `services/risk/`, `services/scheduler/`, `services/qc_adapter/`, `services/reconciliation/`. Future dispatcher PR (Week 4 work; separate forbidden-whitelist PR) wires `plan_calibration_fit` into the monthly cron + bootstrap call site.
  - **Per-market OLS, NOT portfolio-level.** Per backend-spec §2.14 functional form `slippage_bps_market = alpha_market + beta_market * (order_size / ADV_30d)` — the spec text explicitly tags both intercept + slope with `_market` subscripts. Hence `CalibrationPlan.per_market_coefficients: Mapping[str, MarketCoefficient]` rather than a single (alpha, beta, r_squared) on the plan. This also matches the §3.12 schema column `per_market_coefficients JSONB` shape `{"MES": {...}, "ES": {...}, ...}` exactly — the audit payload + the JSONB row will be byte-identical for chain-verifier reconstruction. The `plan_calibration_fit` briefing-shape sketch was orientation-only ("don't copy verbatim; read §2.14 first") and the briefing's single-alpha-beta hint was a simplification; the real spec contract requires per-market.
  - **MIN_OBSERVATIONS_FOR_OLS = 30 applied per market, not portfolio-wide.** Per spec §2.14 "first real calibration after 30 days of Phase 1 LIVE fills" — interpreted as "30 fills per market" rather than "30 fills total" because the regression is per-market. This means the dispatcher's monthly cron will fan out across markets and a market that hasn't hit 30 fills yet keeps its zero-prior coefficients (alpha=0, beta=0) rather than fitting on a small noisy sample. The two-tiered fallback (per-market + plan-level) is documented in the calibration.py docstring's "Two fit methods" block.
  - **Plan-level fit_method label = `OLS` when ANY market crosses MIN_OBSERVATIONS_FOR_OLS threshold, else `ZERO_PRIOR`.** The first-iteration logic checked `r_squared is not None` to detect OLS, but that fails on the constant-x degenerate branch (Sxx=0 → r_squared=None even though n_obs >= 30). The fix is to use `n_obs >= MIN_OBSERVATIONS_FOR_OLS` as the threshold-crossing signal. This caught one failing test (`TestNonBootstrapFallback::test_mixed_market_one_ols_one_fallback_labels_plan_ols`) which exercises a real-world edge case (one market has 30 identical-size fills, another has 5 fills — MES should be OLS-labeled even though the regressor is constant; ES is zero-prior; plan label is OLS because at least one market ran through the estimator). Discussed in calibration.py's `plan_calibration_fit` body comment.
  - **Slippage sign convention: `+bps = cost to trader`.** For a buy, `+slippage_bps` means the realized price was higher than expected (trader paid more); for a sell, `+slippage_bps` means the realized price was lower than expected (trader received less). Both fold into a single signed Decimal via the direction multiplier. This is the standard finance convention and matches what spec §2.14's functional form implies (a positive beta on size/ADV means larger orders cost more to execute — only makes sense if `+slippage_bps` is "cost to trader"). Unit-test fixtures in TestSlippageSignConvention lock all 4 sign permutations.
  - **Decimal-only OLS arithmetic, no float promotion.** Following dev-guide §3.8 + A05: every step of the regression — `xbar`, `ybar`, `Sxx`, `Sxy`, `Syy`, `beta`, `alpha`, `r_squared` — uses Decimal operations (sum + product + division). Decimal default precision is 28 digits which is more than enough for the bps-scale numbers we're fitting on; tests TestOLSFit confirm the hand-computed `y = 2 + 4x` returns `beta=4, alpha=2, r_squared=1` to 4 decimal places. Float promotion would risk subtle accumulator drift across the 30+ fills × 5+ markets the dispatcher will call this with monthly. The cost is `Decimal` is slower than `float` by ~10× for arithmetic — irrelevant for a once-per-month cron over ≤1000 fills.
  - **Caller-supplied `new_version_id` (UUID) rather than planner-generated.** The planner does NOT call `uuid_v7()` directly. The caller passes in a UUIDv7 that flows into both the `CalibrationPlan.new_version_id` AND the audit event payload's `new_version_id` field. This keeps the planner pure (same inputs → same plan) and matches the recon precedent where `account_id` + `contract_id` are caller-side. The dispatcher will generate the UUID once before invoking the planner, use it for the audit write + the row INSERT — both rows reference the same UUID end-to-end.
  - **Caller-supplied `parent_version_no` for monotonic increment, no DB read in the planner.** The planner returns `version_no = parent_version_no + 1` (or 1 if None). The caller-side dispatcher reads `SELECT max(version_no) FROM slippage_calibration_versions` BEFORE invoking the planner and passes the result in. This is symmetric with the existing `services/qc_adapter/poll.py` cursor pattern and keeps the planner DB-free. A small race exists between read-max + insert-new — the table's `version_no UNIQUE` constraint catches it (the INSERT raises a uniqueness violation if a concurrent calibration grabbed the same number) and the dispatcher retries with the new max. For Phase 1 this is a non-issue — calibration is a monthly cron, single-writer.
  - **`becomes_head` default True with explicit False reserved for "compute-but-don't-promote" research.** The Phase 1 monthly cron + bootstrap should default to swapping the HEAD pointer to the new version (the operational norm). A future Phase 1+ research path may want to compute calibration coefficients without promoting them (e.g., shadow-fit comparisons, A/B testing). Setting `becomes_head=False` writes the audit + row but leaves `is_head=FALSE` on the new row. The partial unique index doesn't fire because no row has `is_head=TRUE` set in the swap — the existing HEAD stays HEAD. The audit payload includes `becomes_head` so the chain verifier can distinguish "this calibration was promoted" from "this was a research compute".
  - **Iterable input + internal tuple consumption.** `plan_calibration_fit(fills=...)` accepts `Iterable[FillObservation]` (not just tuple/list) so callers can pass generators. The planner immediately consumes into a tuple (`fill_tuple = tuple(fills)`) so it can be iterated multiple times for window math + per-market grouping + OLS fit without re-iteration concerns. TestIterableInput locks this contract (a generator input produces the expected fill_count + data_window).
  - **Greek-letter convention: ASCII-only in comments + docstrings.** Initial draft used Greek symbols (`α`, `β`, `Σ`, `×`, `²`, `x̄`, `ȳ`) for math notation in docstrings + comments. Ruff RUF002 + RUF003 + RUF004 flag these as "ambiguous Unicode" because they look like ASCII letters in some fonts (`α` could be confused with `a`). The project's pyproject.toml ruff config doesn't ignore these rules, and no other in-tree file uses Greek letters in comments. Replaced with plain-English equivalents (`alpha`, `beta`, `xbar`, `ybar`, `Sxx`, `Sxy`, `Syy`, `**2`, `*`) — the math reads identically and stays grep-able. The pattern of "ASCII-only in comments + docstrings" is now the de facto repo convention; future PRs adding math-heavy comments should follow.

- **Pre-implementation scope-triage finding:** IG line 375 says "Scaffold Next.js frontend in `frontend/`" — but `Docs/claude-dev-guide.md` §2.1 (Repo Layout) + the existing `CLAUDE.md` file-index + the existing `apps/web/.gitkeep` placeholder ALL authoritatively use `apps/web/`. Per dev-guide §1 authority precedence ("specs win on architecture; this guide wins on implementation patterns; repo layout is implementation"), `apps/web/` wins. Day 20 PR ships the IG text correction inline at line 375 (`frontend/` → `apps/web/`) rather than carrying it as a separate doc-only follow-up PR — bundling matches the dev-guide §1.2 "one logical change per commit" lock because the IG line is part of the same Day-20-scaffold logical change.
- **Three small live-discovered fixes during pnpm/docker smoke:**
  - **Next 14.2.20 → 14.2.35 (Dec 2025 security advisory).** First `pnpm install` succeeded but emitted `WARN deprecated next@14.2.20: This version has a security vulnerability. Please upgrade to a patched version.` Per `npm view next@14 dist-tags`, the `next-14` tag points to 14.2.35 (latest patched in the 14.2 line). Bumped both `next` and `eslint-config-next` to 14.2.35 with exact pins (no `^`/`~` per the brief lock). `pnpm install` post-bump removed the deprecation warning. The remaining `WARN deprecated eslint@8.57.1` is informational only — `eslint-config-next` 14.2.x is pinned to ESLint 8 by design, and bumping ESLint would require bumping `eslint-config-next` which requires bumping Next, which is out of scope.
  - **`addgroup: gid '1000' in use` on `node:20-alpine` runtime stage.** First Dockerfile mirrored the api Dockerfile pattern (`groupadd --gid 1000 trading + useradd --uid 1000 --gid trading`) which works against `python:3.11-slim` because that base image doesn't have a 1000 user. `node:20-alpine` already ships the `node` user at uid/gid 1000 (the canonical Node Docker non-root convention). Two options: (a) reuse the existing `node` user, (b) bump trading to uid/gid 1001. Chose (a) — matches the canonical Node Docker pattern + still satisfies the dev-guide §3 / backend-spec §8.11 non-root requirement + avoids cosmetic uid 1001 drift from the api/webhook_pusher pattern (the COPY `--chown=node:node` lines make the intent obvious in the Dockerfile).
  - **Docker build context = `apps/web/`, not repo root.** First build sent 315MB of build context up front (entire repo trees including `node_modules/`, `.next/`, `services/`, `tests/`, `Docs/`, `secrets/`, the existing api + webhook_pusher containers' image layers, etc.). The api + webhook_pusher Dockerfiles use `context: .` because they need repo-root-relative paths (`pyproject.toml`, `services/`, `infrastructure/`, `strategies/`, `alembic/`). The nextjs container only consumes `apps/web/` source — there's no benefit to repo-root context, just cost. Switched `docker-compose.yml` `nextjs.build.context` from `.` to `apps/web` and the Dockerfile's COPY paths from `apps/web/package.json` to `package.json` etc. Build context dropped to apps/web subtree (still includes node_modules + .next from the local dev install, but apps/web/.dockerignore filters those out — apps/web's .dockerignore now applies correctly because it sits at the context root). When Phase 1+ adds `packages/api-types` as a shared TypeScript dependency, the context bumps back to repo root + a top-level `.dockerignore` will need to exclude services/* tests/* etc. — flagged as a Day 21+ follow-up only if/when that dependency lands.

When Day 22 wires real SSE + TanStack Query + Recharts onto the Today page, the cold-start budget has plenty of headroom.

The pattern is repeatable for any future "ship a service, then onboard it to CI" sequence.

**Tests + CI:** unchanged on the Python side. 604/604 tests green. On the frontend side, PR #67's CI run is the first invocation of `frontend-test` in GitHub Actions and the first invocation of `docker build (web)` — both passed in 1m9s / 1m19s respectively. Future PRs will have both gates active.

A27 does NOT bind for the doc-only PR itself (no third-party platform contract added or changed in the documentation).

New npm deps: `@simplewebauthn/browser@13.1.0` + `qrcode@1.5.4` + `@types/qrcode@1.5.5` (frontend QR rendering — backend doesn't ship QR images per spec §5.2 "frontend just renders QR"). **Tests:** 88 new Python tests — `tests/unit/test_auth_helpers.py` (35: CeremonyStore + TOTP enrollment/verify/encryption/key-loading + backup-code generation/normalize/verify/Argon2id); `tests/unit/test_auth_webauthn.py` (15: locked constants + registration/authentication challenge shapes + verify-error paths); `tests/unit/test_auth_routes.py` (26: TestClient + dependency_overrides for all 12 endpoints, happy + error paths); `tests/integration/test_auth_session_lifecycle.py` (12: real Postgres 16 via testcontainers — create/load/refresh/revoke/idle-expiry/strength-upgrade/UV-stamp + cookie encoding round-trip). Combined: 73 unit + 12 integration = 85 new auth tests; 614 → 702 total passing locally. **Anti-patterns:** A22 BINDS for the integration test (real Postgres triggers + grants + column types). A06 enforced (every timestamp tz-aware UTC). A05 N/A (auth code doesn't touch money). A01/A04 N/A by execution (no audit_log writes in Day 21 surface — the `WEBAUTHN_REGISTERED` / `WEBAUTHN_LOGIN` / `TOTP_LOGIN` / `BACKUP_CODE_USED` event types are already in the §3.30 taxonomy from Day 8 and the route handlers will call `append_audit_event` when the risk dispatcher wires up Phase 1 Week 4 Wed). A18 enforced (no bare-domain strings; `webauthn_rp_id` defaults to `localhost` for dev, sops-overridden for paper/live). A27 BINDS for two new third-party platforms: WebAuthn (browser `navigator.credentials.*` + `py_webauthn` server lib + `@simplewebauthn/browser` client lib) + AES-256-GCM via `cryptography.hazmat.primitives.ciphers.aead.AESGCM` (already transitive via webauthn dep). Day 21 PR commit message references the CI-time smoke per dev-guide §6.8 alternative (a): `pytest tests/unit/test_auth_helpers.py tests/unit/test_auth_webauthn.py tests/unit/test_auth_routes.py + pnpm build in apps/web/ + docker compose build api nextjs`. **Operator E2E ceremony at Day 24 Fri** closes the full A27 contract (touches the real authenticator hardware).
- **Substantive design decisions captured in code:**
  - **In-memory `CeremonyStore` (per-process, single-replica) for Phase 0 + Phase 1.** A single asyncio.Lock-guarded dict carries 30-min setup-ceremony state across the 4 wizard steps + 5-min login-ceremony state across the WebAuthn challenge → verify round-trip. Documented Phase-2 cutover path: when api fleet scales to multi-replica, the store moves to Redis or a small Postgres table keyed by `ceremony_id`. Route handlers depend on the public `CeremonyStore` interface, not the dict — drop-in replacement.
  - **`auth_strength: weak ↔ strong` is the APPLICATION-LAYER mapping** over the schema's `sessions.totp_bootstrap_only BOOLEAN` column (backend-spec §8.5.3 column name; frontend-spec §5.4 label). `weak ↔ TRUE` means TOTP-only login or backup-code recovery — risk-loosening blocked until WebAuthn UV. The in-place upgrade path per frontend-spec §5.4 LOCKED is wired via `sessions_mod.upgrade_strength_to_strong` (atomic single-statement UPDATE flipping `totp_bootstrap_only = FALSE` + stamping `last_uv_at_utc`).
  - **TOTP secret AES-256-GCM column encryption with sops-sourced key.** The 32-byte key ships via `secrets/<env>.enc.yaml` (`totp.encryption_key`) and is base64url-decoded at boot via `services.api.auth.totp.load_encryption_key`. The DB stores `nonce(12 bytes) || ciphertext+tag` as BYTEA; tampered ciphertext surfaces as `cryptography.exceptions.InvalidTag` and is wrapped to 503 by the route handler. Backend-spec §8.5.2 "separate from sops" is interpreted as "separate KEY from the sops master age key, but still distributed via sops at rest" — the standard column-encryption pattern.
  - **Backup-code display format LOCKED to 10-char base32 `ABCDE-FGHIJ` per dev-guide §1.5 + frontend-spec §5.3.** Server generates via `secrets.token_bytes(8) → base32 → first 10 chars → hyphen-split at index 5`. Effective entropy: 50 bits per code (16-char base32 sliced to 10 = 50-bit address space). Argon2id-hashed at rest (same hasher as `setup_tokens`: time_cost=3, memory_cost=64 MiB, parallelism=4, hash_len=32, salt_len=16). Raw codes returned exactly once via `POST /api/auth/backup-codes/generate` response; subsequent retrieval is impossible.
  - **Setup wizard's verbatim acknowledgment is UX-only.** The "I have saved my backup codes" string is checked client-side (the Continue button stays disabled until typed exactly). Backend doesn't re-validate — it's not a security gate, just an opportunity to make the operator look at the codes one more time before navigating away. Frontend-spec §5.3 LOCKED says "exact case-sensitive match; trim leading/trailing whitespace" — implemented verbatim.
  - **Setup token path stays `/api/setup/verify-token` (NOT under `/api/auth/`)** per Day 5 / Day 15 pattern + spec §4.1.1 line 1710. The endpoint creates the ceremony_session_id that the WebAuthn-register / TOTP-enroll / backup-codes-generate calls consume via body — NOT via cookie (since the operator has no session yet). Day 21 keeps the existing endpoint and adds the ceremony-session minting on success.
  - **Endpoint ambiguity: spec wins over IG colloquial name.** IG line 376 says "backend `/api/auth/webauthn/register-begin` + `/register-complete`" but backend-spec §4.1.1 line 1702-1703 says `/api/auth/webauthn/register/challenge` + `/register/verify`. Day 21 implements the spec paths verbatim per dev-guide §1.3 ambiguity protocol (specs win on contract; IG informal prose loses).
  - **Session middleware integration: `SessionStubMiddleware` STAYS.** The Day 15 stub path remains active in `dev` + `paper` envs (FAILS CLOSED in `live-*`). Day 21 builds real session creation + cookie issuance ALONGSIDE the stub via `services.api.auth.sessions.create_session`; the route handlers issue cookies on successful WebAuthn-verify / TOTP-verify / recover, but the read-side middleware in `services/api/session.py` is NOT yet updated to read from the real `sessions` table. That swap-in is deferred to a focused Day 22+ session (the operator still gets a working ceremony today and the cookie is set; downstream protected endpoints work via the stub for now). When the swap-in lands, `SessionStubMiddleware` is replaced wholesale with a real cookie-reader that calls `sessions_mod.load_active_session`.
- **What's NOT in Day 21 (deferred per the brief's out-of-scope list):**

  - SSE client (`apps/web/src/lib/sse.ts`) (Day 22 Wed)

  - `/system` Account section "Regenerate backup codes" UI (Phase 1+)
  - Reader role + CPA invite flow (Phase 3 per spec §5.7-§5.9)

The 3 new pages (`/setup`, `/login`, `/recover`) have functional TS strict + ESLint + `pnpm build` coverage but no runtime unit/component tests. **This is a Day 22+ follow-up** — operator E2E ceremony at Day 24 (Fri per IG line 379) is the canonical integration test for Day 21's frontend work; component tests are nice-to-have but not required to close Week 6 verification gate box 1.

### Day 21 verdict

Day 21 [CLAUDE_CODE] split into two PRs per dev-guide §1.2 lock. **PR A** (`feat(alembic): Day 21 — auth tables ...`, merged as PR #69, commit `5b3ff71`) ships the 4 new schema tables (`webauthn_credentials`, `totp_secrets`, `backup_codes`, `sessions`) with a 10-test alembic up→down→up smoke proving the migration is reversible + the column shapes match backend-spec §8.5.1/§8.5.2/§8.5.3 verbatim — `risk-review-approved` label applied per §2.2. **PR B** (`feat(auth): Day 21 — WebAuthn + TOTP + backup-code ceremony on /setup + /login + /recover`, merged as PR #70, commit `4966060`) ships ~1850 lines across 17 files: 5 new backend modules under `services/api/auth/` + 1 new repo + 19 extended schemas + 12 endpoint routes + 3 new frontend lib modules + 3 page replacements + 88 new tests (73 unit + 12 integration + ~3 in test_auth_helpers' module contract class). Combined: full WebAuthn / TOTP / backup-code ceremony surface per backend-spec §4.1.1 + frontend-spec §5.1-§5.3 + §2.8.1-2.8.3 + dev-guide §1.5 LOCKED rules. All gates green locally: `pytest` (702 passed, was 614), `ruff format --check` (147 files clean), `ruff check` (clean), `mypy --strict` (101 source files, zero errors), `dep-drift-check` (19 runtime deps), `pnpm typecheck` (clean), `pnpm lint` (✔ No ESLint warnings or errors), `pnpm build` (12 routes, /setup 112 kB First Load JS, /login 102 kB, /recover 99 kB), `docker compose --env-file deploy/.env.example build api nextjs` (both images built clean). **A02 BINDS on PR A** (alembic/**); A22 BINDS for the 12 integration tests in PR B (real Postgres 16 testcontainer for session-row lifecycle); A06 enforced (every timestamp tz-aware UTC); A05 N/A (auth code doesn't touch money); A18 enforced (no bare-domain strings; `webauthn_rp_id` defaults to `localhost`, sops-overridden for paper/live); A27 BINDS for two new third-party platforms (WebAuthn + AES-256-GCM column encryption) — satisfied at CI-time via the test contract + Docker build, fully closed at operator Day 24 E2E ceremony per IG line 379. **No `services/risk/**`, `services/signal/**`, `services/audit/**`, `services/execution/**`, `services/reconciliation/**`, `services/calibration/**`, `services/agent/**` touched by either PR**; PR A touches only `alembic/**`. **PR B touches `services/api/**` (hot-fix whitelist) + `apps/web/**` (regular review) + `pyproject.toml` + `services/api/Dockerfile` + 4 new test files; no `risk-review-approved` label needed for PR B.** Day 21 closes **Week 6 verification gate box 1 at the test-and-contract level** (the operator-browser E2E half lands at Day 24 per IG line 386 — "Navigate to `https://<your-domain>/setup` → complete WebAuthn registration → navigate to `/login` → authenticate with WebAuthn without password → land on Today page").

**Day 21 carryover items DEFERRED to bundle with Day 24 Fri operator ceremony** (operator decision recorded 2026-05-13 immediately post-merge of PRs #69 + #70). The three carryover items are:

1. **Sops secrets update** — `secrets/paper.enc.yaml` needs two new blocks (`totp.encryption_key` = base64url-encoded 32 random bytes for AES-256-GCM column encryption; `webauthn.rp_id` = `spratcapital.com` + `webauthn.rp_name` = `trading-system` + `webauthn.origin` = `https://spratcapital.com`). Plus the `services/api/entrypoint.py` env-var mapping (sops yaml keys → `API_TOTP_ENCRYPTION_KEY` + `API_WEBAUTHN_RP_ID` + `API_WEBAUTHN_RP_NAME` + `API_WEBAUTHN_ORIGIN`). **Operator action required** — only the operator holds the age key for sops decrypt/encrypt; the entrypoint mapping can land in the Day 24 carryover PR.
2. **VPS deploy** — operator-delegated SSH to Hetzner Ashburn (`root@178.156.239.84`) per the Day 12 → 14 → 15 → 16 → 17 → 20 carryover pattern: `git pull --ff-only && docker compose build api nextjs && docker compose up -d`. Captures the bootstrap setup token via `docker compose logs api 2>&1 | grep SETUP_TOKEN` (operator stores out-of-band — print + 1Password note).
3. **Day 24 carryover doc PR** — single doc-only PR bundling sops update + entrypoint mapping + VPS deploy + two-surface smoke (loopback `/setup` + external `https://spratcapital.com/setup`) + operator E2E ceremony (Touch ID + 1Password TOTP + fireproof safe for 8 backup codes) + the Week 6 verification gate box 1 close-out.

**Rationale for the deferral:** Day 22 work (Today page real content per IG line 377) runs in the local dev environment against mock data — it does NOT depend on the Day 21 ceremony being live in production. The `SessionStubMiddleware` Phase-0 stub continues to serve protected endpoints in `dev`/`paper` env (FAILS CLOSED in `live-*`) so the Day 22 frontend can iterate without needing a real session cookie round-trip. Bundling the Day 24 ceremony with the operator's end-of-week ceremony slot saves a deploy cycle + the operator can prepare physical hardware (Touch ID device + 1Password backup vault + fireproof safe) in one focused session instead of two.

**Hard gate before Day 24 ceremony can run:** the sops update MUST land first (otherwise TOTP enrollment 503s on `TOTP_KEY_MISSING` and WebAuthn registers against `localhost` which Touch ID will refuse). The VPS deploy chains off the sops update. The doc PR is the final step.

**Operator constraint until Day 24:** **DO NOT navigate to `https://spratcapital.com/setup` in any browser**. The bootstrap setup token is single-use + the WebAuthn registration cannot complete against the placeholder `localhost` rp_id. Any accidental visit consumes the token and forces a backend restart to mint a fresh one (token regen happens automatically on api boot per `_bootstrap_owner_token` in `services/api/main.py`, but it's an avoidable round-trip).

Day 22 (Week 6 Wed — `[CLAUDE_CODE]` Today page real content per IG line 377) is the next [CLAUDE_CODE] target — separate session, fully unblocked. **Day 21 verdict status: DONE on code; Day 24 ceremony deferred per operator.**

- **Substantive design decisions captured in code:**
  - **Single `useSSE()` mount at page level (`page.tsx`) rather than per-component.** Spec §4.1 LOCKED is "single multiplexed channel: `GET /api/sse/events`" — the backend's N=4 tab cap also applies per-user across all browser tabs. Mounting `useSSE` from multiple components would open multiple SSE connections from a single tab and rapidly hit the N=4 cap as the operator opens additional tabs. Future pages (Trades, Performance, System) should NOT re-mount `useSSE` — the hook's invalidations on every event are global to the QueryClient, so the Today-page mount serves all visible pages.
  - **Cache-invalidation map (sse.ts `INVALIDATE_KEYS`) mirrors spec §8.6 verbatim.** Every event type lists the TanStack Query keys it invalidates. `ping` + `session_evicted` + `job` + `version` are control events with empty arrays (no business-data invalidation). Phase 0 SSE produces only `ping` events (no real `signal`/`fill`/`position` emitter until Week 4 Wed dispatcher); but the wiring is in place so the invalidation cascade works end-to-end when data lands.
  - **`session_evicted` envelope terminates the SSE connection.** Server-side the multiplexer closes the connection after sending `session_evicted`; client-side `sse.ts` catches the envelope, fires a P1 toast (or P0 for `breakglass_kill`/`creds_rotated`), and transitions to terminal `evicted` state — no auto-reconnect since the server explicitly evicted this tab. Operator must refresh manually to reconnect.
  - **Approve/Reject/Defer buttons call the 501-stub Day 15 endpoints, not nothing.** The mutations fire real network requests; the `SIGNAL_HANDLER_NOT_WIRED` ApiError surfaces a P2 toast explaining the handler isn't wired yet. This proves the end-to-end button-click → mutation → API → toast loop today, so when the Week 4 Wed dispatcher PR lands the handlers, the buttons work with zero frontend changes. Alternative (greying out the buttons until Phase 1) was rejected — operator should see the canonical error envelope today as the contract.
  - **Phase-0 `DecisionDiaryEntry` placeholder for reject/defer.** The Day 15 schema requires a `decision_diary_entry` body on reject/defer with min 10 char `reasoning_text`. Spec §3.1 ships the full DecisionDiaryModal in Phase 1. Day 22 sends a constant placeholder (`manual_judgment` tag + the literal "Phase-0 placeholder — DecisionDiaryModal lands Phase 1 (frontend-spec §3.1).") so the button click reaches the 501 stub end-to-end. The Phase-1 modal replaces the constant with a real form.
  - **Exposure cluster limits are hard-coded placeholders.** Spec §2.2.2 D shows the bars rendering against ring/cluster limits from the risk envelope. The `/api/system/risk-envelope` endpoint is a Phase 1+ wiring (not in Day 15 scope). Day 22 hard-codes the limits from the §2.2.1 layout example (`equity_idx: 60%, rates_bonds: 80%, commodity: 80%, crypto: 40%, fx: 60%`, `gross: 300%`, `net: 150%`). When the endpoint lands the component swaps to reading from a new `useRiskEnvelope()` hook; Day 22 ships the visual + interaction layer.
  - **No Zustand stores Day 22.** Spec §8.4 recommends Zustand for "UI-only state that doesn't belong in query cache" — cmdk-open, drawer stack, toast IDs, filter state. Day 22 has none of these: cmdk-open is a Phase-1 CommandPalette component; drawer is Phase-2; toasts are managed by the new `toast.ts` module-level store (lighter than Zustand for the single-mount Toaster case); filters are per-page Phase-1. Wiring an empty Zustand provider today adds a dep without exercising the contract. Day 23+ revisits when a real UI-only state need emerges.
  - **No `decimal.js` install.** Spec §8.10 reserves `decimal.js` for unavoidable frontend arithmetic. Per §8.9 LOCKED, the backend computes everything; Day 22 only displays pre-computed strings. Phase 0 returns Decimal-as-string `"0"` payloads which `format.ts` renders via native `Number(x).toFixed(2)`. The `decimal.js` install lands Day 22+ when a component actually needs to subtract/sum strings on the client (e.g., a future "select N rows to compute total P&L" feature).
  - **`@microsoft/fetch-event-source` chosen over native `EventSource`** per spec §4.1 — native cannot send custom headers (`Last-Event-ID` is the only allowed one), cannot expose connection lifecycle for backoff control, uses opaque auto-retry. Day 20 already installed the dep; Day 22 wires it.
  - **`useToasts` via `useSyncExternalStore` not Zustand.** The toast queue is a textbook external-store case: single global subscriber (the `<Toaster>` mount), imperative `toast(...)` callers from any component, no per-component reactive selectors needed. `useSyncExternalStore` is React's canonical primitive for this pattern; pulling Zustand in just for this would be overkill.
  - **`'use client'` directive on every component except `layout.tsx`.** The 8 Today-page components all use hooks (TanStack Query + the toast/SSE store) so they MUST be client-side. `layout.tsx` stays server-side (it has no hooks); the client boundary lives at `<Providers>`. This matches the Next.js 14 App Router idiom + dev-guide §4.3.
  - **Module-level singleton QueryClient (not `useState(() => new QueryClient())`).** Per `apps/web/src/lib/queryClient.ts` module export. TanStack docs recommend a stable client across renders; the alternative `useState` pattern survives StrictMode double-render but is friendly to per-test instance injection (future Vitest fixture). Day 22 chose the simpler singleton; the Day 23+ test framework can introduce a `Providers` prop for test-injection without breaking call sites.
  - **No optimistic updates Day 22.** Spec §8.5 LOCKED optimistic UX needs a real server handler for the round-trip — otherwise every approve flickers between "optimistically approved" and "501 rolled back". When the dispatcher lands (Week 4 Wed in implementation calendar; ahead of frontend's Week 7 Mon real-data swap), the optimistic-update pattern layers on without breaking the existing `useApproveSignal` / `useRejectSignal` / `useDeferSignal` call sites.

    - `docker-compose.yml` — `discord_bot` service moved OUT of `phase1` profile (Day 23 brings it up by default); `build` context `.` + `dockerfile: services/discord_bot/Dockerfile`; `networks: [internal, egress]` (internal for `http://api:8000`; egress for `gateway.discord.gg` WSS); `environment` block declares `ENVIRONMENT` + `LOG_LEVEL` + `API_BASE_URL` (the three `DISCORD_*` secrets come via the sops bundle volume + entrypoint mapping); `volumes` mounts `${SECRETS_DIR:-/opt/trading/secrets-decrypted}:/run/secrets:ro` for the bundle; `depends_on: api.condition: service_healthy` so the bot doesn't try to call api before api's `/api/health` returns 200.
    - `.github/workflows/ci.yml` — `docker-build` matrix gains `discord_bot` entry (`dockerfile: services/discord_bot/Dockerfile`, `context: .`) — mirrors the api + webhook_pusher pattern, NOT the web entry's apps/web-context. Day 23 + Day 24+ PRs touching `services/discord_bot/**` get the bot image rebuilt in CI as a smoke check.
    - `pyproject.toml` — ruff per-file-ignores extended: `"services/discord_bot/entrypoint.py" = ["S603", "S606"]` (exec()s a known argv) + `"services/discord_bot/commands/*.py" = ["B008"]` (decorator stack pattern — same exception as FastAPI routes).

- **What's NOT in Day 23 (deferred per the brief's out-of-scope list):**
  - VPS deploy (bundled with Day 24 Fri ceremony per PR #71 deferral lock).
  - Live operator smoke (Day 23 carryover OR Day 24 ceremony).
  - Phase 1 slash commands (`/pnl`, `/exposure`, `/calendar`, `/last-fills`, `/ratify`, `/health`, `/vacation start`, `/close`, `/diary`, `/audit`, `/today`).

  - SSE-driven bot embed posts (backend → bot push pattern lands Phase 1).
  - Per-trade Discord threads (Phase 2 per spec §6.2).
  - `/halt` resume path (locked web-only per dev-guide §1.5 + spec §6.1; bot returns `RISK_LOOSENING_FORBIDDEN_FROM_DISCORD` error for resume attempts — this is enforced by the backend, bot doesn't even register `/resume`).
  - `dpytest` or live-runtime Discord tests (intentional deferral; pure-Python embed + command tests give 73-test coverage; live operator smoke fills the runtime-contract gap).
- **Decision: path naming `services/discord_bot/` (underscore) over `services/discord-bot/` (hyphen).** Spec dev-guide §2.1 + §2.3 listed `discord-bot/` (hyphen) but Python's import system cannot import dotted names containing hyphens. The two paths to make hyphen work — (a) separate `pyproject.toml` inside `services/discord-bot/` with `src/discord_bot/` layout, or (b) `sys.path` manipulation — both add non-trivial CI complexity (separate pip install + module-path manipulation) for zero functional benefit over the underscore directory. The existing scaffold at `services/discord_bot/__init__.py` was already underscore. The IG line 383 reference was already underscore. The `webhook_pusher/` + `qc_adapter/` precedents use underscore. Day 23 lands `services/discord_bot/` AND fixes dev-guide §2.1 + §2.3 inline to match — same surgical 3-char change as Day 20 PR #66's `frontend/` → `apps/web/` precedent (small doc fix bundled with the implementing PR).
- **Decision: api-side `BotAuthMiddleware` runs OUTERMOST.** The middleware order after Day 23 becomes `BotAuth → SessionStub → RequestContext → RateLimit → CSRF → routes`. BotAuth must run before SessionStub because in live envs SessionStub fail-closes (HTTP 401 `AUTH_REQUIRED`) on requests without `request.state.session`. By the time the bot's bearer reaches the api, BotAuth must already have injected the service-account session — otherwise SessionStub 401s the bot's call before BotAuth's session population can apply. BotAuth must also run before CSRF because CSRF reads `request.state.is_bot_authenticated` to decide whether to skip the cookie+header double-submit check for bot calls (the bot has no cookies; forcing CSRF would mean two secrets to manage).
- **Decision: lazy-import `SessionContext` inside `BotAuthMiddleware.dispatch`.** The natural import path (`services.api.middleware` → `services.api.session` → `services.api.config` → `services.api.middleware`) forms a cycle at module-load time because `services.api.session.SessionStubMiddleware` already imports `services.api.config.APISettings`. Lazy-importing `SessionContext` inside the dispatch method (which runs only after the module graph has fully settled) breaks the cycle without restructuring either module. Safe at request time; not safe at module-load time.
- **Decision: bot's `/halt` confirm view 60s timeout + invoker-only + consumed-once.** The 60s window is long enough for a deliberate "read the side-effects, then click" pause; short enough that a stale ephemeral message after operator distraction doesn't stay clickable. Invoker-only `interaction_check` is defense against shared-guild scenarios (single-operator system today; future-proofs against a junior-operator addition or shared-guild assistant). Consumed-once flag guards against fast double-clicks racing two api calls — discord.py disables the view on the first click but the race window exists.
- **Decision: tests target `POST /api/system/kill-switch/invoke` for the bot-auth integration coverage.** The invoke handler depends only on `get_session_context` (no DB-touching repo dep), so unit tests can exercise the full middleware chain (BotAuth + CSRF bypass + SessionStub injection-or-skip + route handler reaches its 501 stub) without spinning up Postgres or a testcontainer. The other repository-coupled endpoints (`/api/system/kill-switch` GET, `/api/positions/current`) would need `app.dependency_overrides[get_session]` wiring; that's a Phase 1 integration-test concern when the dispatcher lands the real handler.
- **Tests + CI:** 714/714 unit tests pass locally (was 641 baseline + 73 new: 44 embeds + 15 commands + 14 bot auth). `make ci` end-to-end green: ruff format-check + ruff check clean + dep-drift-check 19/19 + mypy --strict 110 source files clean + pytest 31.51s + pnpm typecheck/lint/build clean. `docker compose --env-file deploy/.env.example build discord_bot` succeeds cleanly (Phase 1 will produce `ghcr.io/shaanyp123/trading-discord-bot:latest`). **A22 N/A** (no Postgres tests; bot doesn't write Postgres directly — calls api over HTTP). **A27 BINDS + satisfied** for Discord platform: `deploy/discord_bot/README.md` operator runbook + CI-time `docker compose build discord_bot` (per dev-guide §6.8 alternative (b)) + Day 23 carryover live smoke. **A18 enforced** (zero bare-domain strings; `API_BASE_URL` env var threaded through; bot token + guild ID + api-bearer all sops-encrypted). **A01/A04 N/A by execution** (no audit-chain writes; bot reads via api HTTP). **A05 N/A** (Decimal handling at api boundary only; bot renders strings). **A06 enforced** (every datetime in `embeds._require_aware_utc` is checked tz-aware UTC; `astimezone(_ET)` converts cleanly).
- **Smoke-tested via:** `python3 -m pytest tests/unit/test_discord_bot_embeds.py tests/unit/test_discord_bot_commands.py tests/unit/test_api_bot_auth.py` (73 tests in 5.76 + 2.68 + 2.11s); `make ci` (full gate; 31.51s pytest + the four other gates green); `docker compose --env-file deploy/.env.example build discord_bot` (multi-stage build complete; image pre-runtime import sanity check passes against 8 expected symbols + `discord.__version__`). Live Discord-side smoke (`/status` + `/positions` + `/halt` confirm in the operator's dev guild) lands at Day 23 carryover OR Day 24 Fri ceremony per the deferral lock.
- **Result:** Day 23 [CLAUDE_CODE] PR ready to merge. **Week 6 verification gate box 3** (In Discord: type `/positions` → bot responds with positions embed (empty or mock data)) is **CLOSED at the test-and-build-contract level**. The live-Discord half lands at Day 23 carryover OR Day 24 Fri operator ceremony (bundled per the Day 21 + Day 22 + Day 24 carryover deferral lock in PR #71). Day 24 Fri (`[OPERATOR]` E2E ceremony per IG line 384) is the next target — separate session, bundles three carryovers (sops update for `discord.api_bearer_token` + `totp.encryption_key` + `webauthn.*` keys; VPS deploy advancing from PR #66 commit `80a2d47` to current `main` head; live E2E ceremony for `/setup` + `/login` + Today page + Discord bot `/status`+`/positions`+`/halt`).

### 2026-05-15 — Day 23 post-merge — Day 21 entrypoint carryover (4 missing sops yaml → env-var mappings)

- **Spec reference:** `implementation-guide.md` §3 Week 6 Tue Day 21 status (line 380) + Day 24 Fri pre-ceremony status (line 385) — both flag that the Day 21 `services/api/entrypoint.py` env-var mapping (sops yaml keys → `API_TOTP_ENCRYPTION_KEY` + `API_WEBAUTHN_RP_ID` + `API_WEBAUTHN_RP_NAME` + `API_WEBAUTHN_ORIGIN` env vars) was deferred from Day 21 to the Day 24 carryover. `services/api/config.py` (Day 21 — `totp_encryption_key: SecretStr | None`, `webauthn_rp_id`/`rp_name`/`origin` with placeholder defaults). `services/api/entrypoint.py` (Day 23 added the parallel `discord.api_bearer_token` → `API_DISCORD_BOT_BEARER_TOKEN` mapping at line 74-81; Day 21 mappings still missing on `main` post-Day-23 merge per audit at PR #73 close).
- **Decision:** open a tiny carryover PR adding the 4 missing mappings BEFORE the operator runs the Day 24 Fri ceremony. The api would otherwise boot in production with the sops-encrypted `webauthn.*` + `totp.encryption_key` values present in `/run/secrets/decrypted.yaml` but ignored because no env-var mapping exists — TOTP enrollment would 503 `TOTP_KEY_MISSING`, WebAuthn would register against placeholder `localhost` rp_id, and the operator's ceremony would fail mid-flight. Same defensive degradation pattern as Day 23's `API_DISCORD_BOT_BEARER_TOKEN` (skip if env var preset; skip if sops value missing or `<TODO_*>` placeholder; non-fatal — downstream code surfaces the missing-key case at runtime).
- **Files shipped (one-commit PR `feat(api): Day 21 carryover — wire TOTP encryption key + WebAuthn rp_id/rp_name/origin sops mappings`):**
  - `services/api/entrypoint.py` — 4 new `if "API_X" not in os.environ` blocks added between the Day-23 discord block and the `API_VERSION` block. Each block mirrors the watchdog mapping shape: `_looks_like_placeholder` guard + skip-when-missing. The mappings:
    - `API_TOTP_ENCRYPTION_KEY` ← `totp.encryption_key`
    - `API_WEBAUTHN_RP_ID` ← `webauthn.rp_id`
    - `API_WEBAUTHN_RP_NAME` ← `webauthn.rp_name`
    - `API_WEBAUTHN_ORIGIN` ← `webauthn.origin`
  - `tests/unit/test_api_entrypoint.py` — 5 new tests across the existing module-level test surface: `test_main_extracts_discord_bot_bearer_when_present` (defensive backfill for the Day-23 mapping; previously untested at the entrypoint layer); `test_main_extracts_totp_encryption_key_when_present`; `test_main_extracts_webauthn_triplet_when_present`; `test_main_does_not_override_preset_day21_env_vars` (env-var-preset takes precedence over sops — same docker-compose `environment:` override pattern as the watchdog bearer); `test_main_skips_day21_env_vars_when_sops_key_placeholder` (`<TODO_*>` values are NOT exported; per-key independence — placeholder `rp_id` + real `rp_name` exports only the real one).
- **What's NOT in this PR:**
  - The operator's sops update (still operator-only at Day 24 session start — Claude doesn't have the age key).
  - VPS deploy (operator-delegated at Day 24).
  - The bundled Day 21 + 22 + 23 carryover doc PR — this PR is the minimal pre-ceremony unblock; the broader carryover doc + operator-facing runbook update lands at Day 24 close after the ceremony executes.
- **Tests + CI:** 5/5 new tests pass locally + existing 9 entrypoint tests still pass (14/14). `make ci` end-to-end green: ruff format-check + ruff check clean + dep-drift-check 19/19 + mypy --strict + pytest + pnpm typecheck/lint/build. **A22 N/A** (no Postgres tests). **A27 N/A** (no third-party platform contract — this is internal sops yaml → env-var plumbing). **A06 N/A** (no datetimes). **A05 N/A** (no Decimal). **A18 enforced** (no bare-domain strings; `webauthn_rp_id` defaults to `localhost` per Day-21 config; production override via sops). **No `risk-review-approved` label needed** — `services/api/**` is on §2.3 hot-fix whitelist; pure mechanical entrypoint extension.

### 2026-05-15 — Day 24 ceremony — [OPERATOR + CLAUDE_CODE] full E2E enrollment + Discord smoke + 3 mid-ceremony bug fixes

- **Spec reference:** `implementation-guide.md` §3 Week 6 Fri (line 384): `[OPERATOR] Complete /setup flow in browser: navigate to https://<your-domain>/setup; enter setup token (Claude Code generates one-time token); register WebAuthn credential (Touch ID or hardware key); set up TOTP backup in authenticator app (1Password recommended); download backup codes and store in fireproof safe alongside age key. [BOTH] Test /halt test-reason in Discord — confirm kill-switch state transitions to HALT_NEW and back.` Bundles Day 21 + Day 22 + Day 23 carryovers per the PR #71 deferral lock; closes Week 6 verification gate boxes 1 + 2 + 3 at the LIVE level. Pre-ceremony unblock: PR #74 (`feat(api): Day 21 carryover — wire TOTP encryption key + WebAuthn rp_id/rp_name/origin sops mappings`) landed before the operator started this session.
- **What happened (timeline, ~3 hours wall-time):**
  - **Step 1 — Sops update (operator on local Mac):** Added 5 new keys to `secrets/paper.enc.yaml` per Day 21 + Day 23 prep: `totp.encryption_key` (base64url 32 random bytes), `webauthn.rp_id=spratcapital.com`, `webauthn.rp_name=trading-system`, `webauthn.origin=https://spratcapital.com`, `discord.api_bearer_token` (urlsafe 32 random bytes). Initial commit landed as `ea369d9 chore(secrets): Day 24 ceremony — add ...`. Rotated immediately after Claude leaked both secret values to chat via a `grep` whose output piped to stdout — `1b242de chore(secrets): rotate totp.encryption_key + discord.api_bearer_token (leaked in chat log)` matches the Day-1 QC token rotation precedent. Discord bypass message from main-branch protection is informational (`enforce_admins=false` per `Docs/decisions-log.md` 2026-05-05 Day-1 branch-protection entry; admin direct-push is the documented exception for sops-encrypted file edits).
  - **Step 2 — VPS deploy (operator-delegated SSH to Hetzner Ashburn):** `git pull --ff-only` advanced from PR #66 `80a2d47` (Day 20 baseline) → `1b242de` (3 days of merged PRs + the sops rotation). On VPS, `sops -d secrets/paper.enc.yaml` initially failed because `SOPS_AGE_KEY_FILE` is not auto-set in non-interactive SSH (the export in `~/.bashrc` only runs for interactive shells); recovered by `export SOPS_AGE_KEY_FILE=/etc/credstore.encrypted/age_key` then re-running. Decrypted to `/opt/trading/secrets-decrypted/decrypted.yaml`. `docker compose build api nextjs discord_bot` in 77.9s. `docker compose up -d` brought all 6 containers to healthy (api + nextjs + postgres marked `(healthy)`; webhook_pusher + discord_bot + caddy `running` without Phase-0 healthchecks). Bootstrap setup token captured via `docker compose logs api ... > /root/setup-token.log` and operator transferred to 1Password Secure Note then `shred -u`'d the file. **Alembic state**: pre-existing `20260513_auth_tables (head)` already on the VPS Postgres — Day-21 PR #69 migration must have been pre-applied at some earlier deploy; no explicit `alembic upgrade head` was needed during Day 24. Confirmed via `SELECT tablename FROM pg_tables WHERE tablename IN (...)` returning 4 rows.
  - **Bug 1 (deploy/Caddyfile) — fixed mid-ceremony as commit `9492973 fix(caddy): allow 'unsafe-inline' on script-src ...`:** Operator opened https://spratcapital.com/setup, page rendered correctly, but the token input field would not accept typing. Browser Web Inspector console showed 5 `Refused to execute inline script because it violates the following Content Security Policy directive: "script-src 'self'"` errors. Root cause: Next.js 14 App Router emits inline `<script>` tags for the React hydration bootstrap (`__next_f.push(...)` chunks inlined into the HTML response); the Day-5 Caddyfile CSP `script-src 'self'` blocked them, so the HTML rendered but React never attached → all form handlers were dead. **Fix:** added `'unsafe-inline'` to the `script-src` directive. Phase-1 follow-up: switch to nonce-based CSP (Caddy injects per-request nonce; Next.js applies it via middleware) for tighter security. The fix deployed via direct edit to `deploy/Caddyfile` (mounted into the Caddy container as a bind volume) + `docker compose exec caddy caddy reload`; the actual config reload didn't fully take via reload alone — required `docker compose restart caddy` to land the new CSP on live traffic. Verified via `curl -I https://spratcapital.com/setup | grep -i content-security-policy` showing the updated header.
  - **Bug 2 (apps/web/src/app/globals.css) — fixed mid-ceremony as commit `a7b3e06 fix(web): theme form inputs for dark mode (@tailwindcss/forms reset fight)`:** After Bug 1's fix, operator could type into the token field but no password dots appeared. Screenshot showed the input rendering with a white background; operator's other dark-mode pages use `bg-bg-base #0a0a0a` (very dark) — so the input's inherited `text-text-primary #fafafa` (near-white) text was rendering white-on-white. Root cause: `@tailwindcss/forms` plugin (enabled in `apps/web/tailwind.config.ts`) resets all form inputs to a white-bg + dark-text default, overriding the dark theme's `:root { color-scheme: dark; }`. **Fix:** added `@layer base` CSS in `apps/web/src/app/globals.css` that themes all `input[type=text|password|email|number|tel|search|url]` + `textarea` + `select` with `bg-bg-elevated text-text-primary`, plus a `text-text-muted` placeholder. `@layer base` means per-input className utilities still take precedence when intentional. Deployed via `docker compose build nextjs && docker compose up -d nextjs`.
  - **Bug 3 (services/api/routes/setup.py) — fixed mid-ceremony as commit `5084d74 fix(api): verify_token must persist ceremony in CeremonyStore`:** After Bug 2's fix, operator could submit Step 1 (token verify) successfully → page advanced to Step 2 (WebAuthn register) → clicking "Register passkey" returned 401 with "Ceremony session is invalid or expired" within 5 seconds (well inside the 30-min TTL). Api logs showed POST `/api/setup/verify-token` returning 200 with a ceremony_session_id, then POST `/api/auth/webauthn/register/challenge` returning 401. Root cause: the Day-5 `verify_token` route minted a free-floating `ceremony_session_id = str(uuid7_lib.uuid7())` and returned it in the response body, but **never called `CeremonyStore.create_setup_ceremony()`** — the ceremony existed only on paper. Day-21's PR #70 (which added the CeremonyStore + the 4-step wizard endpoints) tested the wizard endpoints with stub ceremony state injected directly into the store; the integration with the Day-5 route was never tested end-to-end. Subsequent wizard endpoints (webauthn/register/challenge + register/verify + totp/enroll-challenge + totp/setup-verify + backup-codes/generate) all use `_get_ceremony(store, body.ceremony_session_id, expected_kind="setup")` which fails with 401 INVALID_CEREMONY_SESSION when the lookup misses. **Fix:** inject `CeremonyStore` via `Depends(get_ceremony_store)` and replace the UUID7 mint with `await store.create_setup_ceremony(setup_token_uuid=row.token_uuid, intended_role=row.intended_role)`. Also dropped the now-unused `uuid_utils` import. Regression test `test_verify_token_persists_ceremony_in_store` in `tests/unit/test_api_setup_token.py` explicitly fetches the returned `ceremony_session_id` from the store + asserts `kind`/`intended_role`/`setup_token_uuid` are populated, locking the handshake going forward. Existing test relaxed length check (CeremonyStore.mint_key uses token_urlsafe(16) ≈ 22 chars vs the prior UUID7's 36) + injects a fresh CeremonyStore per test to avoid singleton pollution. 720/720 unit tests pass (was 719 + 1 new regression).
  - **Step 3 — Browser ceremony (operator on Mac Safari):** After Bug 3's fix deployed, operator re-ran from Step 1 with a fresh setup token (3rd token of the night — the first two were leaked + rotated/invalidated). 4-step wizard completed cleanly: Step 1 token → Step 2 Touch ID register (iCloud Keychain, MacBook Touch ID) → Step 3 TOTP enrolled in 1Password via QR scan → Step 4 8 backup codes printed (fireproof safe alongside age key paper backups per Day-2 sops runbook) + verbatim "I have saved my backup codes" acknowledgment → redirected to `/` (Today page). No console errors. SSE pill rendered Live with sequence_no. Empty-state Phase-0 UX rendered correctly: Health Score "—" (insufficient_data), zero P&L, no positions, no fills, no alerts.
  - **Step 4 — Discord smoke (operator in Discord client):** All 3 Phase-0 slash commands worked as expected. `/status` → green ✓ embed with environment=paper + version=dev + db_connected=true + postgres check ms ✓. `/positions` → blue info embed with empty-state "No open positions" + Phase-0 explanatory note. `/halt Day 24 ceremony smoke test` → confirm view rendered → operator clicked Cancel → "Halt cancelled. System unchanged." → re-issued + clicked Confirm → 501 special-case embed ("⚠️ Kill-switch handler not yet wired" + Week-4-Wed dispatcher pointer). Bot logs `discord_commands_synced command_count=3` + `discord_bot_ready bot_user=Trading System Bot#5325`.
- **Result — Week 6 verification gate boxes ALL CLOSED LIVE (IG line 388):**
  - ✅ Box 1 — "Navigate to https://<your-domain>/setup → complete WebAuthn registration → /login → authenticate with WebAuthn without password → land on Today page"
  - ✅ Box 2 — "Today page loads with at least Health Score '—', empty Positions table, empty Fills feed, and no JS console errors"
  - ✅ Box 3 — "In Discord: type /positions → bot responds with positions embed (empty or mock data)"
- **Process lesson — token-in-chat leak discipline (3 leaks in this single session):** Three times during the ceremony, I echoed secret values to stdout that the operator dutifully pasted back. The first leak was the original `sops -d ... | grep` whose output included the full key values; the operator caught this and rotated immediately. The second was a `docker compose logs api ... | grep SETUP_TOKEN_EMITTED | head -5` pattern where the structlog `SETUP_TOKEN_EMITTED` line includes `raw_token` in its JSON payload — `head -5` printed it to stdout. The third was an api-log grep filtered by `verify-token|webauthn|register|ceremony|invalid` which matched the `SETUP_TOKEN_EMITTED` lines (because of `verify-token` in the path) and surfaced two raw tokens. **Forward rule added to dev-guide §1.5 LOCKED:** anything that might contain `raw_token` / `password` / `api_bearer` / `encryption_key` / `secret_key` / `private_key` patterns goes to a file with `chmod 600` and the verification step is `wc -l` (count only) — never `head`, `cat`, `grep` displayed to stdout. Even for diagnostic visibility. Counts/line-numbers/SHAs are paste-safe; raw values are not. The discipline rule is now: **if I write a grep pattern that COULD match a log line containing a secret, the grep output goes to a file by default — visibility is opt-in via an additional explicit step**. This matches the Day-1 QC token rotation incident precedent and the subsequent Day-3 sops setup discipline (`Docs/decisions-log.md` 2026-05-05 entries).
- **Files shipped in the wrap-up PR (`docs: Day 24 close-out — ceremony complete + viewer-role runbook + leak discipline lesson`):**

Single public entrypoint: `apply_state_transition(*, plan, db, account_id, env, phase_at_emit) -> AppliedStateTransition`. The two-step plan-then-apply pattern is identical to `services.risk.sizing` (Day 6-9 PR #28) and `services.reconciliation.recon` (Day 9 PR #42). **Audit-first ordering** per backend-spec §2.10.1: iterates `plan.audit_events` in order calling `append_audit_event` (each event commits in its own SERIALIZABLE transaction; the writer manages the advisory lock + retry); then opens a separate transaction for the `risk_state` UPSERT (UPDATE prior `is_current=TRUE` row to FALSE, INSERT fresh row with `is_current=TRUE` carrying the LAST audit event's UUID as the FK). Returns `AppliedStateTransition(state_transition_audit_event_uuid, new_state, new_severity)` — Literal-typed for mypy narrowing at the route layer. **Why no SSE emit here:** keeping the risk layer free of api-layer dependencies preserves the option to call the same dispatch entry-point from non-api callers (a future scheduler worker, a Discord bot path that hits the DB directly). The route handler emits SSE after this returns. **A01 BINDS** (every audit row routes through `append_audit_event`). **A02 BINDS** (`services/risk/**`). **A04 BINDS** (every event_type is in the locked taxonomy). **A06 enforced** (all timestamps tz-aware UTC). **A05 N/A** (no monetary values).

### 2026-05-18 — Day 25 carryover — operator-delegated VPS smoke + 2 latent grant/session bugs surfaced + first prod audit row written

- **Spec reference:** Day 25 PR #76 merged earlier in the day; carryover smoke per the Day-12/14/17/24 operator-delegated SSH pattern. Operator authorization: "you take care of it" — Claude drove the deploy + smoke end-to-end via `ssh root@178.156.239.84`, with operator merging the two fix-up PRs (#77 + #78) inline.
- **What happened (timeline, ~50 min wall-time):**
  - **20:08 UTC — initial deploy.** SSH baseline check (`HEAD=5084d74 Day 24 mid-ceremony fix`; all 6 containers healthy; audit_log empty per the standing Day-12 baseline). `git pull --ff-only origin main` advanced `5084d74 → 99c9f2f` (PR #76). `docker compose build api` + `up -d --force-recreate api` brought the new image up healthy in 12s.
  - **20:14 UTC — first invoke smoke: DP-021 surfaced.** `docker compose exec -T -e BOT_BEARER=<extracted-via-Python-stdout-into-shell-var-never-echoed> api curl -sS -X POST .../api/system/kill-switch/invoke` returned **HTTP 500** with `sqlalchemy.exc.InvalidRequestError: A transaction is already begun on this Session`. The failure was in `services/audit/writer.py:238` (`async with session.begin()` in `__aenter__`), which means the api had reached the writer but the session was already in a transaction state — violating the writer's contract at writer.py:58 ("Callers MUST NOT pass a session that already has an open transaction"). **Root cause:** FastAPI's `Depends` cache shares the same `AsyncSession` between `Phase1QueryRepo` reads (`fetch_active_account_id` + `fetch_risk_state_current`) and `apply_state_transition`. SQLAlchemy 2.0 autobegin semantics: the repo's SELECTs implicitly start a transaction; the writer then raised because a transaction was already active. Day-25 unit tests never caught this because they monkeypatched `apply_state_transition` (real writer never ran); Day-25 integration tests used a fresh session not previously touched by a repo. **State post-failure:** `risk_state=NORMAL` preserved; audit chain still empty (the writer raised in `__aenter__` BEFORE any SQL hit). No orphan data.
  - **20:15-20:24 UTC — DP-021 fix authored + tested.** Branch `claude/day25-carryover-dp021-transaction-fix` off `99c9f2f`. Fix: `await db.commit()` between the repo reads and `apply_state_transition` in both invoke + resume routes (`services/api/routes/system.py:336` + `:459`). Regression test in `tests/integration/test_kill_switch_end_to_end.py:test_apply_after_repo_reads_via_same_session_dp021` asserts BOTH directions (without commit → raises; with commit → succeeds). Unit-test fake `_NoopDbSession` gained a no-op `commit()` (route happy path calls it). All 752 tests green locally; lint + mypy clean. **PR #77 opened + operator merged inline at 20:23 UTC.**
  - **20:24-20:26 UTC — second deploy.** Pull `99c9f2f → 1aed311`; rebuild api (10s); restart. Healthy.
  - **20:25 UTC — second invoke smoke: DP-022 surfaced.** Same curl returned **HTTP 500** again, different error: `asyncpg.exceptions.InsufficientPrivilegeError: permission denied for sequence audit_log_sequence_no_seq`. **Root cause:** `alembic/versions/0006_roles.py:84` grants INSERT/SELECT on `audit_log` TABLE to `app_service` but never grants USAGE on the BIGSERIAL backing sequence `audit_log_sequence_no_seq`. The `ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES ... ON TABLES` clause at 0006:88-90 only covers TABLES — sequences need a separate `ON SEQUENCES` grant. Postgres' BIGSERIAL DEFAULT calls `nextval(...)` which requires USAGE on the sequence; without it the INSERT fails with SQLSTATE 42501. **Why this stayed latent for 5+ weeks:** every prior audit-writer integration test binds to the testcontainer SUPERUSER (the default postgres user); SUPERUSER has implicit grants on every object so the gap was invisible. Day-25's PR was the **first production code path** to write to `audit_log` as `app_service` — exposed immediately. Chain still empty (no SQL executed; writer raised in `nextval` before INSERT landed).
  - **20:26-20:34 UTC — DP-022 fix authored + tested.** Branch `claude/day25-carryover-dp022-audit-log-sequence-grant` off `1aed311`. New alembic migration `20260518_audit_seq_grant` (operational, idempotent; revision id kept 24-char to fit Alembic's `VARCHAR(32)` `version_num` column — the longer `20260518_audit_log_sequence_grant` (33 chars) overflowed during initial test run). Migration grants `USAGE, SELECT ON SEQUENCE audit_log_sequence_no_seq TO app_service` + `ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES IN SCHEMA public GRANT USAGE, SELECT ON SEQUENCES TO app_service` (future-proofing). Regression test `test_audit_log_insert_as_app_service_succeeds_dp022` in `tests/integration/test_audit_immutability.py` exercises the writer AS `app_service` via `SET LOCAL ROLE`. Also updated `tests/integration/test_alembic_auth_tables.py` `TestUpDownUp` to target `_AUTH_REVISION` explicitly instead of `head`/`-1` (with DP-022 sitting on top of the auth tables, `head` is no longer `20260513_auth_tables`). **PR #78 opened + operator applied risk-review-approved + merged inline at 20:35 UTC.**
  - **20:35-20:37 UTC — third deploy.** Pull `1aed311 → 7e8d5cf`. First attempted `alembic upgrade head` failed silently — current revision didn't advance — because the api Docker image hadn't been rebuilt yet (the new migration file was on the host filesystem but not in `/app/alembic/versions/` inside the running container). Lesson learned: when an alembic migration lands, the build-then-upgrade sequence is mandatory; `git pull` alone doesn't propagate the file into the container's image. After `docker compose build api && up -d --force-recreate api`, `alembic upgrade head` ran cleanly: `Running upgrade 20260513_auth_tables -> 20260518_audit_seq_grant, Grant USAGE on audit_log_sequence_no_seq to app_service (DP-022 fix)` + post-upgrade `20260518_audit_seq_grant (head)`. SUPERUSER DATABASE_URL constructed in-shell from `POSTGRES_SUPERUSER_PASSWORD` in `deploy/.env` (never echoed; sanity-checked via `${#DB_URL}` byte count only).
  - **20:37 UTC — third invoke smoke: GREEN.** Same curl finally returned **HTTP 200**: `{"risk_state":"HALT_NEW","severity":"routine","halt_reason":"manual_judgment","audit_event_uuid":"019e18c2-19a7-7482-8201-6aa4ac7ef17f","sse_sequence_no":1}`. Post-state GET `/api/system/kill-switch` confirmed: `risk_state=HALT_NEW`, `severity=routine`, `halt_reason=manual_judgment`, `last_transition_utc=2026-05-11T20:37:14.291649Z`, `last_transition_audit_event_uuid=019e18c2-19a7-7482-8201-6aa4ac7ef17f` (FK linkage from `risk_state.audit_event_uuid` → `audit_log.event_uuid` verified). **First-ever production audit_log write.**
  - **20:37 UTC — verify_chain confirms integrity.** `python -m services.audit.verify_chain --env paper` (via app_service URL) returned `CHAIN OK: 1 rows verified` + structlog `verify_chain_passed env=paper rows_walked=1`. Direct row spot-check via `app_service` SELECT confirmed: `sequence_no=1`, `event_type=state_transition_normal_to_halt`, `env=paper`, `prev_hash[0:16]=0000000000000000...` (GENESIS_HASH for first chain row), `record_hash[0:16]=ea9efc0cbdf61571...` (real SHA-256 of canonical payload + prev_hash).
- **Result — Day 25 LIVE-confirmed for the kill-switch invoke surface:**
  - ✅ Wire end-to-end: bot bearer → HTTP 200 → audit chain row + risk_state UPSERT + SSE emit
  - ✅ Audit chain integrity preserved across the first prod write (genesis row + verify_chain pass)
  - ✅ FK linkage between `risk_state.audit_event_uuid` and `audit_log.event_uuid` correct
  - ✅ State persistence verified via GET `/api/system/kill-switch` post-write
- **What's NOT yet smoked (deferred):**
  - **Discord chat-side smoke of `/halt`.** The bot-bearer curl path is the same wire as the Discord bot's `/halt` command (both go through `services/api/middleware.BotAuthMiddleware`), so the embed-rendering path is the only thing not exercised. Operator can run `/halt <reason>` in Discord any time to close this; expected: `🛑 Halt invoked — state: HALT_NEW` + the audit_event_uuid in the embed body. (Note: the system is ALREADY in HALT_NEW from the carryover smoke; a Discord `/halt` will return 409 ALREADY_HALTED until resume lands.)
  - **Browser SSE event consumption.** The route emitted SSE `risk_state` with `sequence_no=1` (per the response body), but no browser was watching `/api/sse/events` during the smoke. Operator-side verification can be done by opening DevTools → Network → `/api/sse/events` → invoking `/halt` in another tab.
  - **Resume path.** No `/resume` Discord command per spec lock (frontend-spec §6.3 + commands/halt.py:19-24 — WebAuthn UV is web-only). The web `/system` page with the canonical RESUME button ships **Day 27 Wed** per IG line 417. Until then, the system stays HALT_NEW — orthogonal to Day 26 (Trades page) per the IG sequence.
- **Three latent bugs discovered + closed (Day 25 + 2 carryover fixes):**
  - **DP-021** (Day 25 PR — fixed by PR #77): FastAPI `Depends` cache shares session between repo and writer → writer raises on `session.begin()` because repo opened implicit tx. Fix: `await db.commit()` between reads and apply. Regression: `test_apply_after_repo_reads_via_same_session_dp021`.
  - **DP-022** (latent since Day 3 / alembic 0006 — fixed by PR #78): `app_service` had INSERT on `audit_log` table but no USAGE on the BIGSERIAL sequence. Fix: new alembic migration grants USAGE + sets default privileges for future sequences. Regression: `test_audit_log_insert_as_app_service_succeeds_dp022` (exercises writer as `app_service` via `SET LOCAL ROLE`).
  - **DP-021/022 root-cause pattern (process lesson):** both bugs were invisible to CI because every prior audit-writer test ran against the testcontainer's SUPERUSER role. SUPERUSER has implicit grants on every object + no session-scope contention from FastAPI dependency caching. The first production code path that wrote to `audit_log` as the real `app_service` role inside a FastAPI route exposed BOTH gaps within ~10 minutes. **Forward rule (to add to dev-guide §6 testing patterns):** when a new prod surface writes to `audit_log`, the integration test suite MUST include at least one test that runs the writer AS `app_service` (not SUPERUSER) AND simulates the FastAPI session-sharing pattern (i.e., issue a SELECT through the session before calling the writer). Both regression tests added in this carryover bundle satisfy this rule; the rule itself should be codified before the next audit-writing surface lands (signal mutations at Day 27, recon dispatch at Day 28+).
- **Files in this doc PR (single commit `docs: Day 25 carryover — close-out + DP-021/DP-022 post-mortems + first prod audit row`):**
  - `Docs/decisions-log.md` — this entry.

### 2026-05-19 — Day 26 09:00 [CLAUDE_CODE] Trades page (`/trades`) + detail (`/trades/:id`) + CSV export + `/api/trades` endpoint

- **Spec reference:** `implementation-guide.md` §3 Week 7 Tue (line 417): `[CLAUDE_CODE] Trades page (/trades): filterable summary table with date/market filters; CSV export button; minimal per-trade detail page at /trades/:id (signal, market, direction, status, fill_price, fill_qty, P&L — ensures Discord deep-links don't 404)`. Backend-spec §4.1.2 lines 1766-1767 defines `GET /api/trades` + `GET /api/trades/:id` (Phase 1; the `POST /api/trades/:id/close` + `GET /api/trades/export.csv` siblings in the same table are out of scope per Day 26 brief). Frontend-spec §2.3.1 (filter bar layout), §2.3.4 (Phase-1 minimum detail page), §2.3.5 (state copy LOCKED), §15.1 (CSV LOCKED column ordering).
- **Repo baseline at session start:** main at `435a7f3` (Day 25 carryover close PR #79). Working tree clean. `git log --all --since='1 day ago'` confirmed no in-flight branches touching `/api/trades` or `apps/web/src/app/trades/`. Inspection of `services/api/routes/` confirmed `trades.py` does NOT exist (Day 15 only shipped orders/fills/alerts) — the IG line 416 carryover note that claimed "`/api/trades` already shipped as empty-list at Day 15" was incorrect.
- **Pre-implementation scope-triage (surfaced to operator pre-code):** three scope questions:
  - **Q1 — Does `/api/trades` exist?** Answer: NO. Spec defines it (§4.1.2 lines 1766-1767) but Day 15 didn't ship it. Three options: (A) build it; (B) frontend joins fills + orders client-side; (C) spec is silent — pick A.
  - **Q2 — Does Day 26 write audit rows?** Answer: NO. All 3 sub-tasks are pure READ projections (filterable summary table; CSV export; detail page). A01/A22/A02 N/A. No alembic, no risk, no audit.
  - **Q3 — System HALT_NEW state interaction?** Answer: NONE. Trades page reads the `trades` table; HALT_NEW lives on `risk_state`. Orthogonal.
  - **Operator chose Option A — build `/api/trades` + `/api/trades/:id`.** Single PR, regular review (no `risk-review-approved` label). CSV export = client-side blob (Phase 0 has 0 rows; server-side `/api/trades/export.csv` with hash-chain footer requires populated trades + audit-anchor lookup → defer Phase 1+).
- **Implementation surface (10 files + 3 doc files):**
  - **NEW `services/api/schemas/trades.py`** — `TradeSummary` (13 fields — id, env, market, direction, state, opened_at_utc, closed_at_utc, total_quantity, avg_entry_price, avg_exit_price, realized_pnl_usd, entry_signal_id, managed_by_strategy_version) + `TradeDetail` (21 fields — extends Summary with account_id, realized_commission_usd, dividend_pnl_usd, entry_order_id, exit_order_id, managed_by_version full 40-char, strategy_hash, parameter_set_hash, slippage_calibration_version_id) + `TradesListResponse` (`{trades, next_cursor, has_more}`). All models declare `extra="forbid"` per dev-guide §3. Decimal fields render as strings on the wire (Pydantic v2 default for `Decimal | None`); timestamps as RFC 3339 UTC.
  - **NEW `services/api/routes/trades.py`** — `GET /api/trades` reads active account → repo fetch with from/to/market/state/id_prefix/limit/cursor filters → maps row dicts to `TradeSummary` via `_row_to_summary` (computes 7-char `managed_by_strategy_version` from full 40-char `managed_by_version` per spec §4.1.5b convention). `GET /api/trades/{trade_id}` reads active account → repo fetch by PK → 404 `TRADE_NOT_FOUND` AppError with canonical envelope + `details.trade_id` on miss → maps via `_row_to_detail` on hit. UUID path param parsed by FastAPI at the decorator level (422 on malformed). Three `# type: ignore[arg-type]` per row mapper for Pydantic-coercion-from-`object`-typed-dict-values pattern matching the eventual `fills` mapper sketch in `services/api/schemas/fills.py`.
  - **EXTEND `services/api/repos/phase1.py`** — `Phase1QueryRepo` Protocol gets `fetch_trades_page` + `fetch_trade_by_id` methods. `PostgresPhase1QueryRepo` impl runs partition-pruned `SELECT FROM trades WHERE account_id = :acc` with conditional `AND` clauses per filter; `id_prefix` uses `id::text LIKE :prefix || '%' ESCAPE '\'` with `%`/`_` LIKE-metachar sanitization (UUIDs can't legitimately contain those but defensive escape is cheap insurance). Detail query is `WHERE account_id = :acc AND id = :tid` for defense-in-depth tenant scoping. Both methods use `app_service` role inheriting SELECT grant from alembic 0006.
  - **EDIT `services/api/main.py`** — imports `trades_router` after the existing Day-15 route imports + adds `app.include_router(trades_router)` after the alerts router with a "Day 26 — Week 7 Tue Trades page surface" comment.
  - **EXTEND `tests/unit/test_api_phase1_routes.py`** — added `trades_route` import; extended `_StubRepo` with `trades_page` + `trade_by_id` + `last_trades_filter` + `last_trade_by_id_arg` fields + `fetch_trades_page` + `fetch_trade_by_id` async methods recording the filter dict. Added `TestListTrades` (4: empty-no-account returns empty envelope; filter-passthrough across from/to/market/state/id_prefix/limit including URL-encoded `/MES`; limit>200 422; populated-row maps to TradeSummary with 7-char `managed_by_strategy_version`) + `TestTradeDetail` (5: no-account 404 with canonical `TRADE_NOT_FOUND` envelope + `details.trade_id`; missing-trade 404; malformed-UUID 422 — required `_bind_repo` even though stub isn't called because FastAPI's dep resolver enters generator-based deps before path-param validation finishes; populated TradeDetail shape end-to-end; open_position keeps `closed_at_utc=null`/`avg_exit_price=null`/`realized_pnl_usd=null`/`exit_order_id=null` on the wire). Pure Python; A22 N/A.
  - **EXTEND `apps/web/src/lib/api/types.ts`** — `Trade` + `TradeDetail` + `TradesListResponse` + `TradesQueryFilters` + `TradeDirection` + `TradeState` types mirroring `services/api/schemas/trades.py` 1:1. Decimal fields typed `string`; all props `readonly`.
  - **EXTEND `apps/web/src/lib/api/queries.ts`** — `useTradesPage(filters)` + `useTrade(id)` TanStack Query hooks. `staleTime: 30_000` (list) + `60_000` (detail) — no spec §8.1 row for trades but matched to "trades change on fill/exit/capacity-constraint" cadence (slower than positions, faster than audit-log). `buildTradesUrl` helper assembles query string via `URLSearchParams`; `useTrade` guards empty path-param with `enabled: id.length > 0`. Cache key for list is `['trades', filters]` so each filter combo gets its own cache slot.
  - **REWRITE `apps/web/src/app/trades/page.tsx`** — Day-20 placeholder → real Trades page. `'use client'` + `useState` for 5 filter values + `useMemo` over filters with spread-conditional pattern (avoids `readonly`-prop assignment under `exactOptionalPropertyTypes`) + `useTradesPage(filters)` hook. Filter Card: from/to date inputs + market text + state select + ID prefix text + Clear button + Export CSV button. Table Card: 9 columns matching frontend-spec §2.3.1 layout (Date / Market / Dir / Size / Status / Entry / Exit / P&L / Vers); each row wraps the Date cell in `<Link href="/trades/:id">` with `encodeURIComponent`; P&L gets `pnlSignClass` color tint; Vers shows 7-char short hash in `text-text-muted` mono. Empty state per spec §2.3.5: "No trades yet — will populate once first signal fills". CSV export via client-side `Blob` with 14-column subset of LOCKED §15.1 schema (the columns derivable from `TradeSummary` today); CSV cells go through `csvEscape` (quote-wraps + doubles internal quotes when commas/quotes/newlines present); filename `trades_phase0_<isoZ>.csv`.
  - **REWRITE `apps/web/src/app/trades/[id]/page.tsx`** — Day-20 placeholder → real detail page. `'use client'` + `useParams<{ id: string }>` + `useTrade(id)`. Header with back-link to `/trades` + UUID-mono title. Loading/error/empty/populated states. The 404 path: `error instanceof ApiError && error.errorCode === 'TRADE_NOT_FOUND'` renders a friendly "Trade not found" body (NOT Next's hard 404) with hint linking back to `/trades`. Populated state: 4 Cards — lifecycle (status / opened / closed), Position (qty / entry-price / exit-price), Realized P&L (gross + commission + dividend with sign tint), Strategy & provenance (7-char + full 40-char managed_by_version, strategy_hash, parameter_set_hash, slippage_calibration_version_id, entry/entry-order/exit-order IDs). `DetailRow` helper renders label-value pairs with `border-b border-border` divider.
- **Decisions made under §1.3 ambiguity protocol (pattern-internal):**
  - **CSV export = client-side blob, not server-side endpoint.** Frontend-spec §15.1 mandates `/api/trades/export.csv` with audit-chain anchor footer. Phase 0 trades table is empty + no anchor to anchor against. Building the server endpoint with Phase-0 empty body + footer pointing at the Day-25 first prod audit row (sequence_no=1) would be premature — the audit-anchor lookup itself needs design (server reads chain head + signs the footer via JCS hash). Defer to Phase 1+ once trades populate. The client-side blob with a 14-column subset preserves the locked-schema column ordering so future cutover is mechanical.
  - **`/api/trades/:id` returns 404 + canonical `TRADE_NOT_FOUND` envelope (NOT raw 404).** Per IG line 417 "ensures Discord deep-links don't 404" — but Next's hard 404 IS a 404 page. The right reading: the frontend should render a graceful "Trade not found" body INSTEAD of Next's hard 404 page. Backend returns canonical envelope so the frontend can detect via `error.errorCode === 'TRADE_NOT_FOUND'` and render the friendly body.
  - **No-active-account also returns 404 `TRADE_NOT_FOUND` on detail path.** Spec doesn't say — but if no account exists, no trade can possibly exist with the requested ID. Matches behavior of the list endpoint (returns empty list when no account). Alternative (`409 NO_ACTIVE_ACCOUNT`) felt wrong because the operator's question is "show me trade X" and the canonical answer is "no such trade."
  - **`id_prefix` filter Phase-1 lock.** Spec §4.1.2 lists it; Phase 2 command palette is the consumer. Lock the wire now so the palette doesn't trip a contract change later. Treat as ordinary string + escape LIKE metachars defensively.
- **Out of scope (deferred to Day 27+):**
  - `POST /api/trades/:id/close` (re-auth gated; involves risk dispatch + audit write — Phase 2 per spec)
  - `GET /api/trades/export.csv` (server-side; audit-chain anchor footer — Phase 1+)
  - TanStack table virtualization (`@tanstack/react-virtual`; spec §2.3.2 says >50 rows; Phase 0 has 0 rows)
  - Drawer-preferred detail view (Phase 2 per spec §2.3.4)
  - Decision diary linkage on detail page (depends on `/api/decision-diary` enrichment — Phase 1+)
  - Multi-select market filter populated from `instrument_metadata` (Phase 1+; Phase-0 ships a single-value text input)
  - Live operator smoke + browser deep-link test (deferred to Day 26 carryover OR bundled into Day 28 Thu `[BOTH]` paper round-trip when trades populate)

  - `services/api/routes/trades.py` (NEW)

  - `make dep-drift-check` — 19 runtime deps match between pyproject.toml + Dockerfile
  - `make typecheck` — `mypy --strict` 113 source files clean

  - Bundle sizes: `/trades` 3.2kB / 117kB First Load JS; `/trades/[id]` 3.62kB / 115kB First Load (ƒ Dynamic — UUID-parametrized)
- **Anti-pattern audit (dev-guide §11):**
  - **A01 N/A** — no audit_log writes (Day 26 is a read-only projection).
  - **A02 N/A** — zero forbidden-path touches (services/api/** + apps/web/** are both on regular review or hot-fix whitelist).
  - **A04 N/A** — no new audit event types.
  - **A05 enforced + passes** — Decimal as string throughout the wire (Pydantic v2 `Decimal | None` serializes as string by default; verified in `test_populated_row_maps_to_summary_with_short_hash` + `test_populated_row_maps_to_detail_shape`).
  - **A06 enforced + passes** — all timestamps tz-aware UTC throughout; no naive `datetime.now()`.
