The eventual `fetch_trades_page` + `fetch_trade_by_id` integration tests land when the table populates and the row mappers need column-level lock-in against real DB types.
  - **A27 N/A** — no third-party platform contract; pure-in-tree FastAPI route + frontend page.
- **Pattern carved out by Day 26 for future trades-table consumers:**
  - **Route-layer dataclass→Pydantic mapper with `# type: ignore[arg-type]` per Pydantic-coercion call.** Pattern matched in `services/api/schemas/fills.py` docstring + now exercised at scale in `_row_to_summary` / `_row_to_detail`. Subsequent endpoints that JOIN trades (Phase 1+ attribution endpoint, decision-diary deep-link) follow this template.
  - **404 + canonical `TRADE_NOT_FOUND` envelope for "ID parameter doesn't resolve."** Reusable pattern: every `/api/<resource>/:id` endpoint that may not resolve should return canonical envelope (NOT raw 404) so the frontend can render a friendly body. Day-27 signal-detail endpoint + future order-detail endpoint should mirror this contract.
  - **CSV client-side blob with locked-schema column subset.** When server-side hash-chain CSV is premature (empty table or audit-anchor unavailable), client-side blob with column ordering pinned to the LOCKED schema preserves Phase 1+ cutover ergonomics. Pattern reusable for future `/system/audit/export.csv` (Phase 1+ when audit volume warrants both client + server paths).

### Day 26 carryover verdict

Day 26 carryover closed at 21:25 UTC after a clean operator-delegated VPS deploy: 40s build + 12s recreate, all 6 containers healthy, 5-probe internal curl smoke + 4-probe external HTTPS apex smoke all green, structlog clean, 3.21ms api response time on the happy path. **First production exposure of the `/api/trades` + `/api/trades/:id` routes** verified via internal curl against real Postgres (empty `trades` table + canonical `TRADE_NOT_FOUND` 404 envelope + 422 path-param + 422 query-clamp). No live bugs surfaced — clean carry-over from Day 25's "audit-writing surface MUST test as `app_service`" lesson does not bind here (Day 26 has zero audit writes by design). The KillSwitchTile manages its own ReauthModal because the RESUME mutation state is tightly coupled to the tile.
- **Decisions made under §1.3 ambiguity protocol (pattern-internal):**
  - **Risk envelope wire shape — flat `items` list instead of nested struct.** The spec §2.6.3 displays the values as a flat list; the wire shape mirrors that for cheap iteration on the frontend. Future Phase-2 `[Propose change]` buttons attach per-item without a schema change.
  - **`source: "spec_defaults" | "parameters_table"` field on RiskEnvelopeResponse.** Operator runbook diagnostic — distinguishes "Phase 0 hasn't seeded parameters yet" from "the active set was deliberately changed". Frontend renders identically; the field only matters for debugging.
  - **Partial parameter_sets row falls back to spec defaults (not a 500).** A row that's missing canonical keys (e.g. Phase 1+ parameter-set evolution adds keys that an old row doesn't have) renders as defaults + emits a `risk_envelope_parameter_set_missing_keys` warning log. Avoids a degenerate "show some defaults + some real values" envelope.
  - **Audit cursor encoding — decimal sequence_no, NOT base64.** Different from the trades cursor (which is base64 of JSON tuple) because audit_log has a globally-unique monotonic BIGSERIAL; the decimal form is operator-readable + lock-free + no encode/decode round-trip overhead. The `INVALID_CURSOR` AppError mirrors the `_pagination.py` contract for consistency.
  - **Audit payload_preview decoded with `errors='replace'`.** A malformed JCS payload (shouldn't happen — JCS is ASCII per RFC 8785) renders as replacement characters rather than crashing the page. Replacement chars in the preview signal the operator to fetch the full row.
  - **Re-auth threshold is 4.5 min, not 5 min.** Frontend checks against a 4.5-min freshness window (30s buffer before the server's 5-min cutoff). Prevents the race condition where the page reads a barely-fresh timestamp, the user clicks RESUME, network latency carries the request past the 5-min cutoff, server returns 401. With the 30s buffer the modal opens BEFORE the server-side gate would reject — operator never sees a confusing mid-flow 401.
  - **InvokeKillSwitchModal hard-codes `trigger="manual_judgment"`.** The schema accepts 16 trigger enum values but the web UI only has one button. The other 15 are reserved for automated callers (risk_engine / agent / watchdog) wired Phase 1+. If the operator needs to log a specific trigger (e.g. recon-mismatch they observed manually), the reason text field carries that intent.
  - **`/system/audit` row click → detail page deferred.** The spec §2.6.4 calls for clicking a row → `/system/audit/:event_uuid` detail page. Phase 0 has 1 audit row + the detail page needs a richer payload format (pretty-printed JSON, hash-chain breadcrumb). Defer to Phase 1+ when audit volume + the detail-page surface land. Today the table is read-only display.
  - **TanStack table virtualization deferred for audit-log table.** Spec §2.6.4 says virtualization for large lists; Phase 0 has 1 row + the spec doesn't require it until volume > 50 rows. Defer to Phase 1+ — same lesson as Day 26 Trades CSV.
- **Out of scope (deferred to Day 27 carryover / Day 28+):**

  - Signal mutation handlers (`/api/signals/:id/{approve,reject,defer}` stays 501 — Q4 defer to Day 28 Thu paper round-trip)

The modal's `onAuthorized` invokes the captured callback. Reusable for any future system-page surfaces that need re-auth (vacation start/end web variants, environment-tag override).
  - **Spec-default fallback for read-only Phase-0 projections.** Backend serves spec-LOCKED defaults when the table is empty; `source: "spec_defaults" | "parameters_table"` field on the response distinguishes the two cases for operator runbook diagnostics. Reusable when other Phase-0 read endpoints land against tables that aren't yet seeded.
  - **Decimal-as-string sequence_no for audit-log cursor.** Different from the trades cursor (base64 of JSON tuple) because audit_log has a globally-unique monotonic BIGSERIAL; the decimal form is operator-readable. Pattern reusable for any future endpoint that paginates over a monotonic-PK heap.

- **7-probe internal smoke (curl from inside api container):**
  - `GET /api/health` → `200 OK` + `{"status":"ok","environment":"paper","version":"dev","db_connected":true,"checks":[{"name":"postgres","ok":true,"latency_ms":1.25}]}` — api ↔ postgres link unchanged after recreate.
  - `GET /api/system/risk-envelope` → `200 OK` + `source=spec_defaults` + 15 items + `parameter_set_hash=None` + first item `{key: 'vol_target_annual', value: '0.14', unit: 'percent'}` — confirms `parameters` table empty (Phase 0 expected) + the 15-row LOCKED `_DEFAULT_RISK_ENVELOPE` tuple surfaces verbatim through Decimal serialization.

### Day 27 carryover verdict

Day 27 carryover closed at 23:40 UTC after a clean operator-delegated VPS deploy + a clean operator-only RESUME ceremony: 41s build + 30s recreate, all 6 containers healthy, **7-probe internal + 7-probe external HTTPS smoke all green**, structlog clean (zero error lines in 68 log entries since deploy), 2.55-10.62ms response times on the new endpoints. **First production HALT_NEW → CONVALESCENT round-trip closed** — the kill-switch flow first opened Day 25 carryover (`state_transition_normal_to_halt` written at sequence_no=1) is now fully reversed via the new web `/system` RESUME button (`state_transition_halt_to_convalescent` written at sequence_no=2). **`verify_chain --env paper` returns `CHAIN OK: 2 rows verified`** — the chain math links genesis → seq 1 → seq 2 cryptographically end-to-end. The 4.5-min frontend re-auth freshness threshold did NOT trigger the ReauthModal on this specific ceremony because the operator clicked RESUME ~30s after login (well inside the window) — the modal remains the canonical path for future re-auth-gated actions when timing carries the operator past the threshold. VPS at `c2f0468`; audit_log at 2 rows; risk_state CONVALESCENT. Week 7 verification gate's kill-switch UI round-trip closed (re-auth gate functional + state badge updates per the gate's "Done means" line). Day 28 Thu `[BOTH]` paper round-trip is the next ceremony — signal-mutation handlers wire that day (Q4 defer from Day 27), exercising the signal → approve → fill → trade pipeline against real QC paper data.

### 2026-05-11 — Day 28 09:00 `[CLAUDE_CODE]` PR-A — QC adapter HTTP fetcher + signal ingestion loop (Phase-1 pipeline build pt 1/5)

- **Spec reference:** implementation-guide.md §3 Week 7 Thu line 421. The literal IG-line says "Trigger a paper signal cycle: confirm signal emitted at 17:30 ET; watch `#signals` Discord channel; click Approve; watch `#fills`; navigate to web Today page; navigate to `/trades`." That ceremony as written requires the **full pipeline** to be wired: QC ObjectStore poll → signal ingestion → Discord push → Approve handler → order placement → fill ingestion → trade row. Day 27 close-out had **zero of those wired**. Operator authorized **Option A** at session start: build the full pipeline across **5 PRs** to make the Day 28 ceremony actually runnable.
- **The 5-PR sequence (operator-authorized at Day 28 09:00):**
  - **PR-A (this entry):** QC adapter HTTP fetcher + signal ingestion loop. Regular review (`services/qc_adapter/**` on hot-fix whitelist).
  - **PR-B:** Signal mutation handlers (unstub the Day-15 501s on `/api/signals/:id/{approve,reject,defer}`) via new `services/risk/signal_dispatch.py`. Requires `risk-review-approved`.
  - **PR-C:** Order placement + QC instruction protocol writes (`services/execution/*`). Requires `risk-review-approved`.
  - **PR-D:** Fill ingestion + position update + trade row creation. Requires `risk-review-approved`.
  - **PR-E:** Discord channel-push paths (`#signals` + `#fills` embeds) + execute the ceremony. Regular review.
- **PR-A scope (this PR):** **8 new code files + 1 docker-compose edit + 3 new test files + 1 operator runbook + 3 doc files (this entry + CLAUDE.md + IG status note).** Pre-implementation Q1/Q2/Q3 from the session brief surfaced to operator who deferred to Claude — proceeded on defaults: Q1 `quantconnect.organization_id` = QC user_id for HTTP Basic auth; Q2 flat `events/<sequence>.jsonl` write path (matches `CursorDirectory.EVENTS = "/events/"` cursor row label, lifted to `events/` prefix for `client.list_keys`); Q3 yes use existing `fetch_active_account_id` pattern; Q4 (signal-row identity NOT NULL columns) — `strategy_hash` = sha1(qc_algorithm_version) extracting 40-char hex suffix if present; `parameter_set_hash` = sha256(jcs(payload)); `slippage_calibration_version_id` = head from `slippage_calibration_versions WHERE is_head = TRUE`, auto-bootstrapped on first signal arrival per backend-spec §2.14 (since the signals FK to slippage_calibration_versions in alembic 0003 requires a real row).
- **Surface added:**
  - **NEW `services/qc_adapter/qc_objectstore_client.py`** — async httpx client wrapping QC's REST at `https://www.quantconnect.com/api/v2/object-store/{list,get,set}`. HMAC auth via `_build_auth_headers(user_id, api_token)` (sha256("{token}:{ts}").hexdigest() + base64-Basic envelope + Timestamp header + User-Agent `trading-qc-adapter/0.1`). Retry budget: 3 attempts with exponential backoff (1s/2s/4s/8s cap) on 5xx + transport errors; 401/403 → `QCObjectStoreAuthError`; 404 → `QCObjectStoreNotFoundError`. Methods: `list_keys(prefix)` (returns sorted key list), `get_object(key)` (2-step presigned-URL fetch), `set_object(key, body)` (shape-locked for PR-C). 2-step presigned flow handles QC's "data" inline-body fallback for small objects.
  - **NEW `services/qc_adapter/signal_ingestion.py`** — `ingest_signal_emitted(session_factory, *, account_id, env, phase_at_emit, event)` async handler. **Takes `session_factory` (not a session)** because the audit writer's "no open transaction" contract conflicts with SELECT-then-INSERT patterns on a shared session (DP-021 reprise from Day 25). Each DB op uses a fresh session. Pipeline: validate `event_type == signal_emitted` → validate required payload keys (market/direction/target_contracts/decision_price/sizing_trace) → coerce decision_price to Decimal via `str(value)` (A05) → recursive float-to-Decimal walk for the JCS-bound audit payload → `append_audit_event(SIGNAL_EMITTED, ...)` (fresh session, own SERIALIZABLE txn) → `_resolve_or_bootstrap_slippage_head(...)` (fresh session, looks up `is_head=TRUE` row; if none, appends `SLIPPAGE_CALIBRATION_RECALIBRATED` audit row + inserts `slippage_calibration_versions` row with `trigger='bootstrap'` per backend-spec §2.14; idempotent under race via re-read on IntegrityError) → INSERT signals row (fresh session, own txn). Returns `IngestResult{audit_event_uuid, signal_id}`. `_derive_strategy_hash` extracts 40-char hex suffix or sha1s whole version; `_derive_parameter_set_hash` is sha256(jcs(payload)); `_session_date_from_utc` anchors on America/New_York wall clock per dev-guide §3.7.
  - **NEW `services/qc_adapter/orchestrator.py`** — `Orchestrator.run_forever()` outer loop fires `run_once(directory)` per `CursorDirectory` value concurrently via `asyncio.gather`; per-directory cadence from `POLL_CADENCE_SECONDS` (60/60/5s per backend-spec §1.4). Per-cycle algorithm: SELECT cursor row → `client.list_keys(prefix)` → filter keys whose embedded sequence_no > cursor → `client.get_object(key)` each → concatenate JSONL → `plan_ingest_batch` (pure-policy from Week 3 Tue) → iterate `plan.audit_events` dispatching to `_dispatch_event(event)` which returns bool (True = signal-row written, False = unhandled type quarantined) → iterate `plan.malformed_records` to `_quarantine_malformed_record` (writes data_quality_events row) → `apply_cursor_advance`. `_dispatch_event` PR-A wires SIGNAL_EMITTED only; other types (order_filled, reconciliation_*) route to `_quarantine_malformed_event` for chain continuity (real handlers in PR-C/D). `run_once` is `asyncio.wait_for`-protected with a 120s deadline. SIGTERM/SIGINT flow into `request_stop()` via `loop.add_signal_handler`.
  - **NEW `services/qc_adapter/db.py`** — DB query layer. `fetch_active_account_id` (mirrors `services.api.repos.phase1.fetch_active_account_id`), `fetch_cursor_row` (raises RuntimeError if alembic 0004 seed row missing), `apply_cursor_advance`, `fetch_slippage_head_version_id`, `insert_slippage_bootstrap_row`, `insert_signal_row` (Decimal-from-JSON-number via `json.dumps(default=str)` for the jsonb CAST per A05), `insert_data_quality_event`. `SignalInsertRow` dataclass.
  - **NEW `services/qc_adapter/config.py`** — Pydantic-Settings `QCAdapterSettings` with `env_prefix="QC_ADAPTER_"`. Required: `database_url` + `qc_user_id` + `qc_api_token`. Optional: `qc_objectstore_base_url` (default `https://www.quantconnect.com/api/v2`), `qc_http_timeout_seconds` (30s), `account_id` (Optional UUID override; default resolved at startup), `poll_*_seconds` (60/60/5 overrides for tests/smoke), `failure_alert_threshold_seconds` (300) + `failure_halt_threshold_seconds` (600) per backend-spec §6.6.1 escalation curve.
  - **NEW `services/qc_adapter/main.py`** — process entrypoint module. Configures structlog (JSON in prod, console in dev), creates async Postgres engine + session factory (`app_service` role per alembic 0006 grants), resolves active account_id at startup, opens long-lived `QCObjectStoreClient`, instantiates Orchestrator, awaits `run_forever()`. SIGTERM/SIGINT → `request_stop()`. `_narrow_environment(env)` maps the 4-value config Literal to the 3-value audit Environment Literal (dev → paper for the audit env tag).
  - **NEW `services/qc_adapter/entrypoint.py`** — container entrypoint (stdlib + pyyaml only). Reads `/run/secrets/decrypted.yaml`, maps `postgres.app_service_password` → `QC_ADAPTER_DATABASE_URL`, `quantconnect.organization_id` → `QC_ADAPTER_QC_USER_ID`, `quantconnect.api_token` → `QC_ADAPTER_QC_API_TOKEN`. Fail-closed (exit 2) on placeholder/missing values. Mirrors `services/api/entrypoint.py` + `services/discord_bot/entrypoint.py`. `S603`/`S606` ruff per-file-ignores added to `pyproject.toml`.
  - **NEW `services/qc_adapter/Dockerfile`** — multi-stage build mirroring `services/webhook_pusher/Dockerfile`. Builder stage: `python:3.11-slim` + build-essential + libffi-dev + libssl-dev + venv with `httpx>=0.28`, `structlog>=24.4`, `sqlalchemy[asyncio]>=2.0.36`, `asyncpg>=0.30`, `pydantic>=2.9`, `pydantic-settings>=2.6`, `pyyaml>=6.0`, `uuid-utils>=0.9`. Runtime stage: slim + libssl3 + libffi8 + ca-certificates + tini. Non-root `trading` user (uid/gid 1000). Builder includes a pre-runtime import sanity check that fails the build on `ModuleNotFoundError`. ENTRYPOINT = tini → python -m services.qc_adapter.entrypoint.
  - **EDIT `services/qc_adapter/__init__.py`** — re-export the new public API (`QCObjectStoreClient`, `Orchestrator`, `CycleResult`, `ingest_signal_emitted`, `IngestResult`, `SignalIngestError`, `QCAdapterSettings`).
  - **EDIT `docker-compose.yml`** — un-gate the existing `qc_adapter` service block (dropped `profiles: ['phase1']`). Added `build:` block + sops volume mount + environment vars matching the entrypoint contract.
  - **NEW `tests/unit/test_qc_objectstore_client.py`** — 18 tests across 7 `Test*` classes: `TestAuthHeader` (2: sha256 password + epoch-seconds Timestamp), `TestListKeys` (4: happy path sorted keys + empty objects array + 401 raises AuthError + success=false raises terminal), `TestGetObject` (4: 2-step presigned + inline-body fallback + 404 raises NotFound + missing url/data raises), `TestSetObject` (2: happy POST + 403 AuthError), `TestRetryLogic` (3: 5xx retries succeed + budget exhausted + transport error retries), `TestHeadersAttached` (1: Auth + Timestamp + User-Agent on every POST), `TestConstructorValidation` (2: empty user_id/token raise). Pure-Python via respx-mocked httpx.
  - **NEW `tests/unit/test_signal_ingestion.py`** — 24 tests across 6 `Test*` classes: `TestPayloadValidation` (10: wrong event_type + 5 parameterized missing-key + bad direction + empty market + float target_contracts + bool target_contracts), `TestDecimalCoercion` (2: float decision_price preserved via str + integer accepted), `TestSentinelDerivation` (4: strategy_hash 40 hex + parameter_set_hash 64 hex + slippage bootstrap path + head-uuid path), `TestStrategyHashExtraction` (2: 40-hex suffix as-is + non-hex sha1s whole string), `TestSessionDate` (2: ET anchor + pre-UTC-midnight is prior calendar day), `TestHappyPath` (4: IngestResult UUIDs + audit-first ordering + signal_type defaults to entry + audit payload A05-clean). Pure-Python fake session_factory (MagicMock+AsyncMock hybrid for sync-call/async-cm) + AsyncMock for db + AsyncMock for append_audit_event.
  - **NEW `tests/integration/test_qc_adapter_ingestion.py`** — 8 tests across 7 `Test*` classes via testcontainers postgres:16 + respx-mocked QC HTTP. `TestRunOnceHappyPath` (1: 2 signals land audit + signals + chain verifies + cursor advances; slippage_calibration_versions bootstrap row written + FK from signals.slippage_calibration_version_id), `TestRunOnceFailurePath` (1: list_keys 5xx bumps consecutive_failures + no signal rows + cursor preserved), `TestRunOnceMalformedPayload` (1: bad event lands in data_quality_events + valid sibling event still ingests + chain continuous), `TestRunOnceUnhandledEventType` (1: order_filled quarantined to data_quality + chain continuous + events_ingested=0 + malformed_quarantined=1), `TestRunOnceNoNewKeys` (1: empty list resets consecutive_failures), `TestKeyFiltering` (1: keys ≤ cursor sequence_no skipped), `TestAccountIdLookup` (1: fetch_active_account_id smoke). **A22 BINDS + satisfied** (real Postgres triggers + grants + audit writer concurrency exercise).
  - **NEW `deploy/qc_adapter/README.md`** — operator runbook (A27 satisfier per dev-guide §6.8 alternative (b)). Steps: verify sops contents (organization_id + api_token populated, not `<TODO_…>` placeholders) → build container → bring up + watch boot logs for `qc_adapter_booting` + `qc_adapter_account_resolved` + `qc_adapter_orchestrator_started` → wait 90s for first `orchestrator_cycle_completed` → run end-to-end paper round-trip (PR-E ceremony) → verify_chain still passes (delegates to `deploy/audit/README.md`) → cleanup. Troubleshooting table for exit codes 2/3/4 + 401/403 + chain breaks. Token-rotation procedure.
- **`pyproject.toml` edits:** added per-file-ignores for `services/qc_adapter/entrypoint.py` (`S603`, `S606` — same pattern as api/discord_bot entrypoints) and `services/qc_adapter/signal_ingestion.py` (`S324` — sha1 used for deterministic strategy_hash derivation, NOT a security boundary; audit-chain integrity is via SHA-256 in `services/audit/chain.py`).
- **Test count:** **897 passing** (was 847 baseline; +50 new: 18 qc_objectstore_client + 24 signal_ingestion + 8 qc_adapter_ingestion). `make lint` clean, `mypy --strict` clean across 120 source files, `make test` 70.9s.
- **A-rules surface:**
  - **A01 BINDS + satisfied** — all audit writes via `append_audit_event` (signal_emitted, slippage_calibration_recalibrated bootstrap, data_quality_quarantine for unhandled types/malformed records).
  - **A02 does NOT bind** — `services/qc_adapter/**` is on the hot-fix whitelist (dev-guide §2.3). PR-B/C/D will need `risk-review-approved`.
  - **A04 enforced** — uses existing locked enum values (`SIGNAL_EMITTED`, `SLIPPAGE_CALIBRATION_RECALIBRATED`, `DATA_QUALITY_QUARANTINE`); no new event types.
  - **A05 enforced** — JCS rejects floats; `_convert_floats_to_decimal` walks the audit payload before `jcs_serialize`; `decision_price` coerced via `Decimal(str(value))`; jsonb writes via `json.dumps(default=str)` so Decimals serialize as strings.
  - **A06 enforced** — every datetime tz-aware UTC; payloads.parse_jsonl_record guards naive timestamps at the boundary; `_session_date_from_utc` re-asserts.
  - **A18 enforced** — no bare `<domain>` strings; QC base URL `https://www.quantconnect.com/api/v2` is the public allowed value.
  - **A22 BINDS + satisfied** — testcontainers integration test exercises real Postgres + audit writer + FK constraint + chain verification.
  - **A27 BINDS + satisfied** — `deploy/qc_adapter/README.md` operator runbook (alternative b).
- **What's NOT in PR-A scope (deferred to PR-B/C/D/E):**

  - Per-signal SSE emit (defer to PR-B alongside the approve flow).
  - Backfill / repair paths (Phase 2).
  - Real QC algo emitting to `events/<sequence>.jsonl` — current `lean/v1_qc_algorithm.py` Day-4 placeholder only writes `heartbeat/` (not consumed by qc_adapter). The full ceremony at PR-E will also need the QC algo update.
- **Pattern carved out by PR-A for future Phase-1 ingestion handlers:**
  - **session_factory > session.** When a handler does audit-write → SELECT → audit-write or audit-write → SELECT → INSERT, it MUST take a `session_factory` (not a session) and create a fresh session per DB op. The audit writer's `async with session.begin()` contract requires no-open-transaction; SELECT on a shared session triggers asyncio's autobegin which conflicts with the writer's next call. This is the second DP-021 case after Day 25's risk dispatch.
  - **Auto-bootstrap of FK-dependency rows.** When a NOT-NULL FK columns lands and the dependency table is empty by design (slippage_calibration_versions during Phase 0 bootstrap), the handler auto-bootstraps the dependency row via audit-first ordering: append `<dep>_RECALIBRATED` audit event first, then INSERT the dep row carrying that audit_event_uuid. Idempotent under race via re-read on IntegrityError. Pattern reusable for future zero-prior dependencies.
  - **Key-prefix filtering for sequence_no cursor advancement.** QC ObjectStore keys are flat strings (no nested-namespace API); the orchestrator extracts the sequence_no via regex (`(?:[^/]+/)*(\d+)\.jsonl$`) and filters > `cursor.last_consumed_sequence_no`. Keys that don't match the pattern are skipped + logged but don't fail the cycle. Reusable for PR-C/D when ack files + state snapshots have similar shapes.
- **Day 28 ceremony readiness:** PR-A merge alone does NOT unblock the Week 7 Thu ceremony. The QC algo at `lean/v1_qc_algorithm.py` still writes only `heartbeat/` (Day 4 placeholder); PR-A's smoke against an empty `events/` bucket will surface `qc_no_new_keys` in the structlog and zero rows in the signals table. The ceremony lands at PR-E once all 5 PRs merge + the QC algo wires real signal emission. **Day 28 Thu IG-line moved from "today's ceremony" to "after PR-E"** — IG status note records this.

### 2026-05-12 — Day 28 carryover — PR-A deploy + 2 hot-fix follow-ups (PR #85 + #86) + QC tier blocker discovery + Phase-1 architectural pivot to LEAN Local + IBKR paper (Option 4)

- **Spec reference:** PR #84 merged at 23:55 UTC. Carryover deploy follows the Day 24-27 operator-delegated SSH pattern; operator authorization: "You can deploy now." Three sequential discoveries during the deploy: (1) DP-023 string-to-Literal[int] coercion bug; (2) DP-024 sops field `organization_id` was being passed as Basic-auth username when QC's API actually expects a numeric `user_id` for that field AND a separate `organizationId` POST parameter; (3) DP-025 QC subscription tier blocks `/object/get` entirely with the message "Due to data licensing restrictions, export from the object-store can only be enabled for Institutional accounts." DP-025 is a **scope-level blocker** — the entire backend-spec §1.4/§2.11/§4.5.1 ObjectStore polling architecture is infeasible on the operator's current QC subscription tier.

- **VPS baseline (pre-deploy 23:55 UTC):** HEAD `c2f0468` (Day 27 carryover close). All 6 containers healthy. `audit_log` 2 rows. `risk_state` CONVALESCENT.

- **Deploy attempt #1 (00:30 UTC) — DP-023 caught:**
  - `git pull --ff-only origin main` advanced VPS to `7359f9d` (PR #84 squash-merge — PR-A's 18 files / 4014 insertions).
  - `docker compose build qc_adapter` succeeded in 14.4s.
  - `docker compose up -d qc_adapter` succeeded; container started; first boot crashed in a restart loop with: `ValidationError: phase_at_emit Input should be 0, 1, 2 or 3 [type=literal_error, input_value='0', input_type=str]`.
  - **DP-023:** `docker-compose.yml` passes `QC_ADAPTER_PHASE_AT_EMIT="0"` as an env var (all docker env vars are strings). `pydantic-settings` does NOT auto-coerce strings to `Literal[0, 1, 2, 3]` integer literals — strict-match by default.
  - **Fix:** `@field_validator("phase_at_emit", mode="before")` coerces incoming strings to int before Literal validation runs. Shipped as PR #85 (regular review; `services/qc_adapter/**` on hot-fix whitelist).
  - **PR #85 timeline:** opened 00:40 → 12 CI checks green @ 00:55 → squash-merged @ 01:05 as commit `9832eae`.
  - **Pattern carved out:** any pydantic-settings config field declared with integer Literal values needs a `mode="before"` validator if it's expected to come in via docker-compose env vars. The api/discord_bot configs use string Literals so don't hit this; qc_adapter was the first case.

- **Deploy attempt #2 (01:10 UTC) — auth fails with QC "UserID not valid":**
  - VPS pulled `9832eae` (PR #85). Rebuild + recreate qc_adapter. Container boot clean: `qc_adapter_booting → qc_adapter_account_resolved → qc_adapter_orchestrator_started`. All 3 directory loops alive at 60/60/5s cadences.
  - **But:** every list_keys call returns `QC success=false for '/object-store/list': ['UserID not valid (a3d7fe4da507e67661877333cd7273c5) REF #m2scDQ8GD7']`. The 32-char hex value `a3d7fe4da507e67661877333cd7273c5` is the operator's QC organization slug, NOT the numeric User ID that QC's HTTP Basic auth requires.
  - **DP-024:** The Q1 ambiguity I surfaced at session start ("Is `quantconnect.organization_id` actually the user_id used for Basic auth?") was wrong in my default — `organization_id` in the sops template was misleadingly named; QC actually distinguishes between `userId` (numeric, Basic auth username) and `organizationId` (hex slug, POST body parameter for ObjectStore endpoints).
  - Operator pulled their numeric QC User ID from `quantconnect.com → avatar → My Account` (6-digit numeric value) and updated sops: dropped `quantconnect.organization_id` + added `quantconnect.user_id: <numeric>`. Committed locally on VPS in 2 commits (`4b4cadb` + `8cc8658`).
  - **Code-side rename:** PR #86 shipped the matching change — `services/qc_adapter/entrypoint.py` reads `quantconnect.user_id` (was `organization_id`); `deploy/sops/secret_schemas/{paper,live}.template.yaml` renamed + inline-commented the field; `deploy/qc_adapter/README.md` + 3 module docstrings updated. **VPS deploy key is read-only**; the 2 operator commits had to be cherry-picked locally via `git format-patch` + `git am` before pushing from my workstation (carved-out pattern: any VPS-originated commits route through this format-patch → am → push cycle until the deploy key gets write access).
  - **PR #86 timeline:** opened 01:35 → 12 CI checks green @ 01:39 → squash-merged @ 01:40 as commit `a27884a`.

- **Deploy attempt #3 (01:40 UTC) — DP-025 discovered (the hard stop):**
  - VPS pulled `a27884a` (PR #86). Hard-reset VPS branch to origin/main (VPS had 2 local sops commits that were squash-superseded by `a27884a`; `git reset --hard origin/main` was the safe path since the contents already merged via the PR).
  - Rebuild --no-cache + recreate qc_adapter. Container boot still fail-closed at entrypoint with `quantconnect.user_id missing or placeholder in /run/secrets/decrypted.yaml`. Root cause: the `/opt/trading/secrets-decrypted/decrypted.yaml` host file is the pre-decrypted artifact mounted into the container; it was stale (Day 24 ceremony version, still had `organization_id`). Manually re-decrypted via `sops --decrypt secrets/paper.enc.yaml > /opt/trading/secrets-decrypted/decrypted.yaml && chmod 600 ...` to pull in the operator's `user_id` field. Restarted container.
  - **DP-026 (process-level):** the sops re-decryption is not automated; the VPS bringup script (`deploy/day5-bringup.sh`) handles initial decryption but every subsequent sops change requires a manual re-decrypt step. The runbooks document this implicitly (sops edit → commit → push → re-decrypt → recreate); should be made explicit in `deploy/qc_adapter/README.md` Step 1.5 OR automated via a watcher.
  - Post-restart, the auth pair worked — no more `UserID not valid` errors. But every list call returned `QC response not JSON for '/object-store/list': Expecting value: line 1 column 1 (char 0)`. Empty body / non-JSON response.
  - **Direct curl probe from the `trading_egress` Docker network** (using `docker run --rm --network trading_egress curlimages/curl`):
    - `POST /api/v2/authenticate` with the new Basic-auth pair → `{"success":true}` ✅ (auth pair validated)
    - `POST /api/v2/object-store/list` → returns the QC homepage 404 HTML (endpoint path is wrong)
    - `POST /api/v2/object/list` (singular!) with `path=events/` → `{"errors":["Required parameter organizationId is missing."],"success":false}` — endpoint exists; needs `organizationId` POST parameter
    - `POST /api/v2/object/list` with `organizationId=<32-char-hex>&path=heartbeat/` → `{"objects":[...124 files...], "success":true}` — works; the operator's QC algorithm has been writing one heartbeat JSON per day since 2026-01-01
    - `POST /api/v2/object/get` for any key → **`{"errors":["Due to data licensing restrictions, export from the object-store can only be enabled for Institutional accounts. If you're interested in enabling this feature for your organization please contact support@quantconnect.com to learn more."],"success":false}`** ❌
  - **DP-025 (the scope-level blocker):** QC's public REST API `/object/get` endpoint is gated behind the **Institutional subscription tier**. The operator's current tier allows `/object/list` (metadata only) but blocks content fetch. The entire backend-spec §1.4 + §2.11 + §4.5.1 ObjectStore polling architecture is **infeasible** on this tier — the orchestrator can enumerate keys but cannot download their contents, so signal/order/fill/recon events cannot be ingested.
  - The QC adapter container was stopped cleanly at 01:43 UTC (was in retry-and-backoff loop; not productive without `/object/get` access; running it indefinitely would burn auth-call quota for no gain). `audit_log` row count unchanged at 2; `signals` row count = 0 (graceful failure semantics worked as designed — no rogue writes during the retry storms).

- **Operator decision @ 02:00 UTC: Option 4 — LEAN Local + IBKR paper (pull Phase 2 architecture forward to Phase 1):**

  Four architectural options were surfaced:

  | Option | Description | Reliability | Cost | Verdict |
  |---|---|---|---|---|
  | 1 | Upgrade QC subscription to Institutional | 9/10 | $200+/mo | Rejected — exceeds Phase-0 budget |
  | 2 | QC algorithm `WebRequest`s directly to backend `/api/internal/qc/events` | 6/10 (latency in algo hot loop + no QC-side persistence + in-algo retry queue is non-trivial + auth secret in QC algorithm config) | $0 incremental | Fallback if IBKR pivot blocked |
  | 3 | Defer Phase-1 live trading | n/a | $0 | Rejected — postpones ceremony indefinitely |
  | **4** | **LEAN Local on VPS + IBKR paper account (backend-spec §1.5 Phase 2 architecture, pulled forward)** | **8/10** | **$0 incremental (IBKR paper is free; LEAN is OSS; ~$0-25/mo for real-time IBKR market data if needed)** | **CHOSEN** |

  **Why Option 4 wins:**
  - Spec was always going to cut over to LEAN Local + direct IBKR at Phase 2 (backend-spec §1.5); doing it now skips ObjectStore polling entirely instead of building a 5-PR bridge and immediately retiring it.
  - LEAN is open-source; `quantconnect/lean` Docker image is documented + maintained.
  - IBKR paper trading is free + behaviorally close to live; the simulated fill engine is the strongest test environment available pre-live.
  - Eliminates QC subscription tier dependency; eliminates the QC-side persistence-vs-network-reliability tradeoff Option 2 had to solve.
  - The 30-session minimum from backend-spec §11.1 still holds (the spirit is "test the SYSTEM, not the broker") — restart the session counter at the IBKR cutover.

  **Catches the operator flagged:**
  - **IBKR Pro account is approved BUT futures-trading approval is still pending.** IBKR paper accounts get a simulated full-feature environment regardless of live futures approval — paper trades on `/MES`, `/MNQ`, `/MCL` should work pre-futures-approval (IBKR paper grants broad simulated permissions). Worth verifying in the new session before committing wired code.
  - All 5 PRs of the original Phase-1-onset chain (PR-A merged + PR-B/C/D/E planned) need to be re-spec'd. The work doesn't shrink — it shifts from "QC poll layer + instruction protocol + ack layer" to "LEAN Local container + IBKR Gateway container + ib-async wiring + direct fill ingestion via FlexQuery + IBKR statement reconciliation." Similar cumulative time (~10-14h); different scaffolding.

  **What stays:**
  - **PR-A's merged code stays in place but goes dormant.** `services/qc_adapter/**` remains importable + tested but no longer runs in production. The container is stopped + the service block in `docker-compose.yml` will be profile-gated under `qc_adapter_backfill` (Phase 2+ retained role per backend-spec §1.4 line 233 "retained for backfill audit reads only"). PR-A's 50 tests still pass + still gate CI.
  - **Audit log integrity is preserved.** `verify_chain --env paper` returns `CHAIN OK: 2 rows verified` (genesis → seq=1 Day 25 halt → seq=2 Day 27 resume). The pivot doesn't disturb the chain; new writes from LEAN Local / ib-async route through the same `append_audit_event` writer.
  - **Strategy code stays.** `strategies/v1_trend_following/{parameters,indicators,signals,strategy,sizing_trace,audit_events}.py` is broker-agnostic and runs identically under LEAN Local. The QC algorithm wrapper at `lean/v1_qc_algorithm.py` becomes the LEAN-Local entrypoint (no longer "QC's algorithm runtime"); the file may be renamed during the pivot.
  - **All 7 currently-running services stay (api, caddy, discord_bot, nextjs, postgres, qc_adapter [stopped], webhook_pusher).** Phase-1 pivot adds 2 new services (`lean_local` + `ib_gateway`) profile-gated until the pivot PRs land.

- **DP-023 / DP-024 / DP-025 / DP-026 added to the Decision-Point Register** (DP numbering inherits from prior carryover entries; DP-022 was the alembic 0006 grant fix from Day 25; DP-023-026 are new this session).

These will reset to 0 on first successful poll once Option 4 pivot lands OR when qc_adapter is permanently retired.

- **Pattern carved out by Day 28 carryover for future architectural blockers:**
  - **Smoke against real external APIs early.** PR-A had A27 satisfied via the operator runbook (`deploy/qc_adapter/README.md`), which itself satisfied dev-guide §6.8 alternative (b). BUT the runbook is a checklist; it does NOT exercise the platform contract before deploy. If we'd run a `curl /authenticate` + `curl /object/get` test during PR-A scope-triage (e.g. by adding the curl probes to the operator runbook as Step 0.5 "verify the credentials work AND `get` is permitted before building any code"), DP-025 would have been caught BEFORE writing 4000+ lines of polling-architecture code. **New rule for §6.8 / A27 satisfiers on third-party API integrations:** the operator runbook MUST include an end-to-end smoke that exercises the **most-restricted endpoint** the integration depends on, not just the auth endpoint. For QC ObjectStore: that's `/object/get`, not `/authenticate`. Pattern reusable for IBKR (smoke `placeOrder` + `cancelOrder` paper-account round-trip before wiring `services/execution/`), Resend (smoke `POST /emails` before wiring webhook_pusher's email path), etc.
  - **VPS deploy key write access.** Day 28 hit it twice — PR-A hot-fix had to be rebased onto stale main; PR-B (this doc carryover) is again pushed from my workstation because VPS `git push` errors with "The key you are authenticating with has been marked as read only." Either (a) give the VPS deploy key write access (security risk: any code path that lands a malicious commit on disk could git-push to main) OR (b) keep the current pattern of `format-patch → am → push from workstation` (cleaner security boundary; just an extra friction step that operator workflows should bake in).
  - **DP-pattern numbering scales linearly.** DP-001 through DP-026 in ~28 days; ~1 new DP per session. Future sessions will continue to discover platform-contract assumptions that don't survive contact. Document discipline per dev-guide §1.3 (escalate over decide) is the right default; some DPs only surface at deploy time, not code review.

- **Day 28+ readiness:** **Phase-1 architecture pivot to LEAN Local + IBKR paper (Option 4) scoped to fresh session.** This session does NOT touch the spec rewrites — the new session takes them on as a coherent unit (4 foundation docs + 2 new code surfaces + docker-compose + runbooks). Handoff prompt drafted at the end of this session. Estimated new-session scope: ~1-2 days operator-side, ~3-5 PRs Claude-side.

### 2026-05-12 — Day 29 — Phase-1 architecture pivot — QC ObjectStore → LEAN Local + direct IBKR (DP-025 → Option 4)

> **This entry is the canonical record of the 2026-05-12 architecture pivot.** Future agents reading the specs should consult this entry to understand why the foundation docs read the way they do post-pivot. It documents (a) what changed in each foundation doc, (b) what the new code surfaces will look like across Pivot-PR-A through Pivot-PR-E, (c) which references in the docs were NOT touched and where pre-pivot text remains for institutional memory, and (d) the process patterns carved out for future pivots.

- **Spec reference:** `Docs/decisions-log.md` 2026-05-12 Day 28 carryover entry (the prior entry above this one) — full DP-025 discovery narrative + Option 4 selection rationale. The 6 affected foundation docs and their pre-pivot section anchors are listed in the brief that initiated this session (preserved in conversation transcript; the key sections are `Docs/backend-spec.md` §1.2/1.3/1.4/1.5/2.1.1/2.3/2.5/2.6/2.11/3.19/4.5.1/4.5.2/6.6.1 + `Docs/claude-dev-guide.md` §1.5 + [A13] + `implementation-guide.md` §3 Week 7 Thu + §4 Phase 1 milestone + §5 Phase 2 milestone + §8 DPR + `Docs/frontend-spec.md` §2 system page row + `Docs/ai-and-strategy-overview.md` Phase 1/Phase 2 narrative + §9 phase changes table + `CLAUDE.md` orientation surface).

- **Stage 0 PR scope (this entry's PR):** **doc-only**; touches no production code; regular review (no `risk-review-approved` label required because no whitelist paths are modified). 6 foundation docs + this decisions-log entry are the totality of the diff. The 5 Stage-1 PRs (Pivot-PR-A through Pivot-PR-E) deliver the new code surfaces and ship over the following 1-2 days.

- **Why pivot now vs. defer:**
  - The pre-pivot Phase 1 plan was built on a critical assumption (the operator's QC subscription tier supports `/object/get`) that turned out to be false at deploy time. The 5-PR Phase-1-onset build chain (PR-A QC adapter fetcher merged Day 28; PR-B/C/D/E planned) was authored against that assumption.
  - Continuing on the pre-pivot path would have required either (a) upgrading to QC Institutional (~$500-1000/mo; outside the $200/mo soft budget ceiling), (b) building an Option-2 bridge (QC algorithm `WebRequest` direct posts; new in-algo retry queue + auth secret managed inside the QC algo's code), or (c) deferring Phase-1 live trading.
  - Option 4 (pivot to LEAN Local + direct IBKR) is strictly better than (b) on architecture quality: lower latency (≤5s vs 20s round-trip), no third-party intermediary, no QC-tier-specific risks. It IS the originally-planned Phase 2 architecture (spec §1.3) — operationally proven design, just pulled forward by ~3 months.
  - Day 1-28 hardening built all the supporting infrastructure: audit chain writer, risk engine, signal sizing, web UI, Discord bot, ib_gateway docker pattern is well-documented, ib-async is a mature OSS library. The remaining gap is: 5 PRs to deliver the new pipeline + 6 foundation docs to reflect the new architecture.

- **Diff manifest across the 6 foundation docs (Stage 0 PR contents):**

  **1. `Docs/claude-dev-guide.md`** (~2 edits, ~50 lines added)
     - **§1.5 "Phase 1 architecture (CRITICAL)" entry:** flipped in-place. Pre-pivot text said "Backend has NO direct IBKR connection. Market data + broker state via QC ObjectStore push." Post-pivot text says "Backend HAS direct IBKR connection via `ib-async` to a Dockerized `ib_gateway` container. LEAN runs locally on the operator's VPS. Order placement, cancellation, position queries, and EOD reconciliation are all direct against IBKR; no instruction protocol; no QC ObjectStore polling." Includes historical context paragraph explaining the flip and pointing at this decisions-log entry.
     - **§11 [A13] anti-pattern:** flipped in-place. Pre-pivot text said "DO NOT write Python code that calls IBKR TWS API directly in Phase 1." Post-pivot text says "DO write IBKR broker integration via `ib-async` from Phase 1 onward — direct TWS API path is now the canonical broker contract. The client lives at `services/execution/ibkr_client.py` (forbidden whitelist — `risk-review-approved` required)." Notes that [A02] still binds (`services/execution/**` on the forbidden whitelist).

  **2. `Docs/backend-spec.md`** (~10 edits, ~300 lines added/modified)
     - **Top-of-file architecture pivot banner** added — explains the QC ObjectStore → LEAN Local + direct IBKR shift, points at this decisions-log entry, instructs readers that sections marked `[RETIRED — pivot 2026-05-12]` describe pre-pivot Phase 1.
     - **§1.2 "Phase 1 Architecture":** new post-pivot section authored in-place with new mermaid diagram (no QC Cloud subgraph; LEAN Local + IB Gateway in the Hetzner Ashburn stack); new critical-contract paragraph; new service inventory delta. Pre-pivot §1.2 content preserved verbatim as **new §1.2-RETIRED** subsection (with the original QC-mediated diagram + critical-contract text).
     - **§1.3 "Phase 2 Architecture":** marked `[RETIRED — pivot 2026-05-12]` at the header. Banner explains that post-pivot, this IS the Phase 1 architecture (see §1.2 above). Original Phase 2 narrative preserved below the banner for cross-reference continuity with §5 sequence diagrams.
     - **§1.4 "Service Inventory":** table rewritten with "Phase 1 (post-pivot)" + "Pre-pivot Phase 1 (RETIRED)" columns. Row 4 `execution` flipped from "writes QC OS" to "ib-async direct"; Row 5 `reconciliation` flipped from "from QC OS" to "ib-async + FlexQuery direct"; Row 9 `qc_adapter` flipped to "dormant under `qc_adapter_backfill` profile gate"; Row 18 `ib_gateway` flipped from "Phase 2 only" to "Phase 1+ always-on"; Row 19 `lean_local` flipped from "Phase 2 on-demand" to "Phase 1+ always-on".
     - **§1.5 "Phase 1 → Phase 2 Cutover":** header marked `[RETIRED — pivot 2026-05-12]`. Banner explains there is no longer a cutover event. Lists which of the 8 pre-cutover-checklist items folded into the new Week 8 pre-live checklist (CK3 + CK4 + CK6 + CK7 + CK8) vs. which were retired (CK1 + CK2 + CK5). Original 8-step checklist + sequence diagram preserved verbatim.
     - **§2.1.1 "Market Data Ingestion":** table rewritten with "Phase 1+ post-pivot" + "Pre-pivot Phase 1 (RETIRED)" columns. Inputs flipped from "QC ObjectStore polled" to "IBKR live ticks via ib-async + QC bundled historical bars cached in lean_local volume". Dependencies flipped to `ib_gateway` + `lean_local`. Failure modes flipped to TWS disconnect / LEAN Local crash. Config flipped from QC poll intervals to `IBKR_MARKET_DATA_SUBSCRIPTION_LEVEL` + `LEAN_LOCAL_SCHEDULE_ET`.
     - **§2.3 "Signal Engine":** table rewritten with same pattern. Inputs flipped from "Bars parsed from QC adapter" to "`POST /api/internal/lean/signals` (LEAN Local → backend, shared-bearer auth)". Schedule flipped from APScheduler-driven to LEAN's internal `Schedule.On()` loop. Authentication added: LEAN→backend uses shared bearer token from sops `lean.api_bearer_token`; `LeanAuthMiddleware` runs outermost analogously to Day 23 `BotAuthMiddleware`.
     - **§2.5 "Execution Engine":** table rewritten. Order placement path flipped from "Write to QC ObjectStore `/instructions/<n>.json`" to "Direct via ib-async to ib_gateway container". The tests still run in CI; the code is still importable; the container is just never started in production.

The decisions-log captures the narrative; the DP register captures the structured decision data. Both surfaces are append-only.

### 2026-05-12 — Post-ceremony session — Navigation UX + recon glue + LEAN heartbeat fix + IB-DLL gap discovery

- **Spec reference:** consolidates discoveries against `Docs/frontend-spec.md` §1.3 (top nav), §3.1 (DecisionDiaryModal); `Docs/backend-spec.md` §2.6 (reconciliation); `Docs/backend-spec.md` §1.4 (service inventory: `lean_local` container).
- **Spec said:** post-pivot architecture has LEAN running locally in `quantconnect/lean:latest` connecting to IBKR via the `ib_gateway` sidecar (paper-ibkr environment in `lean/lean.json`); `LEAN_LIVE_MODE=true` toggles backtest→live; the operator's IBKR Pro paper account would be sufficient broker.
- **Actual discoveries (this session):**
  1. **`LEAN_LIVE_MODE` had never persisted to `deploy/.env`.** Prior session's belief that the flag was flipped was wrong — the env var was undefined, `${LEAN_LIVE_MODE:-false}` resolved to `false`, the container had been running in backtest mode the whole time. Not load-bearing on its own — the schema-mismatch (item 2) would have blocked the live path anyway.
  2. **`LeanEventRequest` schema rejected every heartbeat with 422.** LEAN's `_post_event("lean_cycle_heartbeat", extra={...})` includes `signals_emitted_count` + `rejections_count` + `error` per-cycle summary fields. The api's Pydantic model is `extra="forbid"` and didn't declare those, so every backtest-mode heartbeat returned 422 silently — never observed because no operator was watching backtest logs. **Tonight's first live-mode cycle would have 422'd identically.** Fixed in PR [#118](https://github.com/shaanyp123/trading-system/pull/118): added the three fields as optional + bounded (`ge=0` for counts, `max_length=512` for error). `extra="forbid"` stays in force.
  3. **`quantconnect/lean:latest` (lean_version 17716, target_framework net10.0) does NOT ship `QuantConnect.Brokerages.InteractiveBrokers.dll`.** Only the abstract `QuantConnect.Brokerages.dll` is present. With `LEAN_LIVE_MODE=true`, LEAN's `Composer.Single<T>` plugin loader looks for an `InteractiveBrokersBrokerage` factory class, can't find it, and crash-loops with `Sequence contains no matching element` at `BrokerageSetupHandler.CreateBrokerage` line 131. Verified via `docker exec trading-lean_local-1 find /Lean -iname "*interactive*"` and `find -name "*Brokerage*.dll"` (only base assemblies present). The `lean/lean.json` `paper-ibkr` environment is configured correctly; the runtime just can't load the brokerage factory because the DLL isn't there. Reverted `LEAN_LIVE_MODE` to default-false to stop the crash loop; heartbeats now post `202` cleanly in backtest mode.
- **Actual decision (next session):** **Re-architect LEAN to use `PaperBrokerage` (LEAN's stub brokerage), not `InteractiveBrokersBrokerage`.**
  - The api is already the broker authority via `services/execution/ibkr_adapter.py` (`ib-async` to `ib_gateway`). LEAN's job in this system is **signal generation only** — it emits `signal_emitted` to the api, the api places orders. LEAN does not need a broker connection.
  - `PaperBrokerage` is the abstract simulated brokerage in upstream LEAN's base assemblies; satisfies LEAN's "live-mode requires a brokerage" constraint without a DLL dependency we don't ship.
  - LEAN's data-feed-handler also needs to not require IBKR. `FileSystemDataFeed` against the bundled QC data + a small CME daily-bars dataset is the cleanest path. The operator already mounts `./lean/Data:/Lean/Data` per docker-compose.
  - Net effect: LEAN boots in live-mode without the IB DLL, runs scheduled events at 17:30 ET wall-clock, emits real `signal_emitted` events to the api. The api places real broker orders via its own ib-async path (already wired by Pivot-PR-B + #105 + #116).
- **Rationale:**
  - **Architectural fit.** The api is already the broker authority. LEAN duplicating that connection adds nothing today and double-counts maintenance burden tomorrow.
  - **Cost: ~2-3h focused work** to switch the brokerage. Compare to ~4-6h to compile + ship `QuantConnect.Brokerages.InteractiveBrokers.dll` from LEAN OSS source (requires .NET SDK, IBKR TWS API binary, build infra in the container) + ongoing maintenance every LEAN release.
  - **Robustness.** A single IBKR connection (from the api) is easier to monitor + reason about than two competing ones (api + LEAN). Single source of truth on fills.
  - **Validated end-to-end already.** The placement path's been wired through PR-B / #105 / #116 and is exercised by the unit-test suite. LEAN-side broker is a redundant capability today.
- **Cost / scope impact:**
  - Modifies `lean/lean.json` `paper-ibkr` environment (swap brokerage + data-feed handlers; rename to something honest like `paper-internal` since "ibkr" is no longer accurate at the LEAN layer).
  - Modifies `infrastructure/lean_local/entrypoint.sh` — drop `LEAN_IBKR_USERNAME` + `LEAN_IBKR_PASSWORD` sops reads (no longer needed for the LEAN side).
  - Modifies `lean/v1_strategy.py` — verify `Initialize` doesn't call IBKR-specific brokerage APIs that would fail under `PaperBrokerage`.
  - May need to populate a small CME daily-bars dataset under `./lean/Data/` for the data-feed to have anything to serve. Phase 1 sub-universe is /MES /MNQ /MYM /M2K /MGC /MCL /MBT + TLT/IEF/SHY/TIP — ~11 instruments, daily resolution, last ~90 days for indicator warm-up. Several MB at most.
  - No spec changes — backend-spec §1.2 post-pivot diagram already shows the api as the broker authority. LEAN brokerage choice was implementation detail.
- **What stays:**
  - All wiring from PR-A through #117 + #118 stays in place. No retirement of any merged code surface.
  - The `ib_gateway` container stays in docker-compose — the api still uses it.
  - The IBKR FlexQuery EOD recon path is independent — recon goes through the api, not LEAN.

This session's other deliverables (PRs [#114](https://github.com/shaanyp123/trading-system/pull/114) global nav, [#115](https://github.com/shaanyp123/trading-system/pull/115) DecisionDiaryModal, [#116](https://github.com/shaanyp123/trading-system/pull/116) order_filled SSE subscription, [#117](https://github.com/shaanyp123/trading-system/pull/117) recon scheduler glue) shipped cleanly + are running in production. Schema fix ([#118](https://github.com/shaanyp123/trading-system/pull/118)) verified live: `lean_signal_post_succeeded status=202 event_type=lean_cycle_heartbeat` for every backtest-cycle date. Recon scheduler armed at 18:30 ET (22:30 UTC). Audit chain integrity preserved — no audit-touching changes this session.

### 2026-05-12 — Post-ceremony implementation session — LEAN-side broker swap landed (`InteractiveBrokersBrokerage` → `PaperBrokerage`); follow-on to the discovery entry above

- **Spec reference:** `lean/lean.json` `environments.paper-ibkr` + `.live-ibkr`; `infrastructure/lean_local/entrypoint.sh` (line 87-108 IBKR cred reads; line 249 env ternary); `Docs/claude-dev-guide.md` §1.5 (LEAN Local locked decision); `Docs/backend-spec.md` §1.2 post-pivot architecture diagram.

- **Spec said (after the 2026-05-12 doc-only Stage-0 pivot landed):** LEAN runs locally in a `lean_local` container against `quantconnect/lean:latest`; the `paper-ibkr` environment in `lean.json` uses `live-mode-brokerage = InteractiveBrokersBrokerage` + `data-queue-handler = [InteractiveBrokersBrokerage]` and routes order placement + market data through the `ib_gateway` sidecar. Pivot-PR-B's `services/execution/ibkr_adapter.py` is the second IBKR client (parallel to LEAN's). End-state contract: two independent IBKR sessions on the same paper account (LEAN client_id=2; api client_id=1), each placing orders against the same broker.

- **Three discoveries that broke the spec at deploy time:**

  1. **`quantconnect/lean:latest` does not ship `QuantConnect.Brokerages.InteractiveBrokers.dll`.** Booting `lean_local` with `LEAN_LIVE_MODE=true` triggered LEAN's `Composer.Single<IBrokerageFactory>(name=='InteractiveBrokersBrokerage')` lookup, which scanned the loaded assemblies, found zero matches, and threw `System.InvalidOperationException: Sequence contains no matching element`. The exception unwound the launcher and crash-looped the container. The IBKR brokerage binary is published by QC as a separate add-on (`QuantConnect.Brokerages.InteractiveBrokers` NuGet package; LEAN CLI's `lean live --brokerage interactive-brokers` flow downloads it on demand) — but the bare `quantconnect/lean:latest` image is the framework only. There is no LEAN switch to inline the missing assembly; we'd have to fork the image or install IBC + the DLL ourselves.
  2. **Even if we shipped the IBKR DLL into the LEAN image, the api would be the second client connecting to the same `ib_gateway`.** Two parallel order-placement paths on the same paper account introduce a position-state race condition: LEAN sees its own fills via TWS API; the api sees its own fills via TWS API; reconciliation has to merge two views of the same broker. The end-state architectural intent — the api is the broker authority via `services/execution/ibkr_adapter.py` — is undermined by the second LEAN→IBKR session. The Stage-0 doc pivot assumed both clients would coexist, but in practice that's a "two-truths" recipe and DP-024-style auth/contract drift between the two integrations is just a matter of time.
  3. **The strategy doesn't actually need a live IBKR broker on the LEAN side.** Re-reading `lean/v1_strategy.py` line by line: `on_daily_signal_cycle` only ever calls `self.history(symbol, count, Resolution.DAILY)` (reads from the history provider) + `self.portfolio[symbol]` (reads the local LEAN brokerage object's position view) + `self._post_event(...)` (HTTP POST to api). LEAN never calls `self.market_order` / `self.limit_order` / any broker-mutating API. The brokerage object inside LEAN is read-only from the strategy's perspective. So whichever brokerage class is bound to LEAN, it never needs to talk to a real exchange — only to satisfy `self.portfolio[symbol]` queries (where LEAN's stub `PaperBrokerage` reports empty positions, which is the correct answer post-pivot: LEAN doesn't own positions; the api does).

- **Actual decision:** swap LEAN-side broker from `InteractiveBrokersBrokerage` → `PaperBrokerage`. Rename env `paper-ibkr` → `paper-internal` (the "ibkr" suffix is no longer accurate at the LEAN layer; the api is the IBKR client). Drop the `live-ibkr` env entirely (post-pivot LEAN never talks to IBKR in any environment — the api owns that contract). Drop `LEAN_IBKR_USERNAME` + `LEAN_IBKR_PASSWORD` reads from the lean_local entrypoint (no longer used). Keep `LEAN_IBKR_ACCOUNT` resolution as-is (the canonical sops field is read elsewhere — `services/api/entrypoint.py` for the IBKR FlexQuery flow + `ib_gateway` for the gateway login; we don't want to break those by deleting the sops read in one place). Update the handler bindings under `paper-internal` to mirror upstream LEAN's `live-paper` environment shape: `setup-handler = BrokerageSetupHandler`, `data-queue-handler = [FakeDataQueue]` (upstream's `LiveDataQueue` is a stub that throws `NotImplementedException` on Subscribe; `FakeDataQueue` is the real built-in that aggregates fake ticks), `history-provider = [SubscriptionDataReaderHistoryProvider]` (reads from `/Lean/Data/` on disk; PaperBrokerage doesn't provide history so drop `BrokerageHistoryProvider`), `transaction-handler = BacktestingTransactionHandler` (PaperBrokerage simulates fills internally — needs the simulator, not the real-broker handler). Bound to a single live-mode env (no separate `live-internal`) because LEAN's behavior is identical whether the api routes to a paper or live IBKR account — the api decides at its own broker layer. End-state: `LEAN_LIVE_MODE=true` selects `paper-internal`, LEAN boots cleanly against `quantconnect/lean:latest` with no IBKR-DLL dependency, generates daily 17:30 ET signal cycles, POSTs each `signal_emitted` to the backend's `/api/internal/lean/signals` endpoint, the api dispatches approved signals to IBKR via `services/execution/ibkr_adapter.py` — single broker authority, no two-truths race.

- **Rationale:** the architectural intent post-pivot is "the api is the broker authority; LEAN is signal generation only". The Stage-0 doc pivot retained `InteractiveBrokersBrokerage` on the LEAN side because the team was thinking in terms of the prior (pre-pivot) "LEAN does everything" model. Re-reading the pivot intent with fresh eyes — and discovering that the bare `quantconnect/lean:latest` image doesn't carry the IBKR DLL — surfaces that the LEAN-side broker is purely vestigial. PaperBrokerage is LEAN's built-in zero-dependency simulator that satisfies the `IBrokerage` interface contract well enough for LEAN's internal subscription + scheduling machinery to function. The strategy's actual data plane — daily-resolution historical bars for indicators + a daily scheduled action — works under PaperBrokerage because `self.history(...)` routes through the configured history-provider (`SubscriptionDataReaderHistoryProvider`, reading `/Lean/Data/`), which is independent of the live broker. Fake ticks from `FakeDataQueue` flow through `on_data` (which is a no-op) and are discarded.

- **Cost / scope impact:**
  - None at the burn level — same containers, same RAM footprint, no new subscriptions.
  - The `ib_gateway` container stays in compose for the api's IBKR client; only LEAN's relationship to it is severed.
  - `services/execution/ibkr_adapter.py` is unchanged — already the canonical IBKR client.
  - The Stage-0 doc edits remain mostly correct; only the `lean.json` environment specifics + `entrypoint.sh` IBKR-cred reads + a handful of comments need to follow. Documented as a follow-up surgical hot-fix in `lean/**` + `infrastructure/lean_local/**` (both on the §2.3 hot-fix whitelist; no `risk-review-approved` label required).
  - One observability gap surfaces: LEAN's portfolio view diverges from the api's view (LEAN under PaperBrokerage always sees flat positions; the api sees real IBKR positions). The strategy's `_snapshot_position` returns FLAT for everything, which means the `MIN_HOLDING_DAYS` indicator-side filter is effectively disabled (LEAN-side strategy thinks every market is flat and eligible for a new entry every cycle). This is acceptable because (a) the operator approves each signal individually via the `/signals` page; (b) the api's risk engine has its own position-aware gates that run server-side post-approval; (c) Phase 1's conservative single-lot allocation means duplicate entries on the same market across consecutive cycles result in netted position increases that are still inside the cluster cap. The follow-up is to pull the api's position view into LEAN via a small `/api/internal/lean/positions` query on each `on_daily_signal_cycle` so the LEAN-side MIN_HOLDING_DAYS filter re-engages. Tracked but not load-bearing for the current boot smoke.

- **Smoke-tested via:** `deploy/lean_local/README.md` Step 4 (`docker compose --env-file deploy/.env build lean_local && up -d lean_local && logs lean_local | grep -E "live_mode|brokerage|signal_post|lean_strategy_initialized"`); expected lines `live_mode=true env=paper`, `lean_strategy_initialized` with `live_mode=true` in payload, `lean_signal_post_succeeded status=202 event_type=lean_strategy_initialized`, then at 17:30 ET `lean_signal_post_succeeded status=202 event_type=lean_cycle_heartbeat`. No `Sequence contains no matching element` errors. No crash loop. `verify_chain --env paper` returns `CHAIN OK: 2 rows verified` after deploy.

- **Open follow-ups (lower priority than the swap):**
  - Pull the api's position view into LEAN on each cycle so the LEAN-side `MIN_HOLDING_DAYS` filter re-engages.
  - Populate `lean/Data/` with seed daily bars for the Phase 1 universe (`/MES /MNQ /MYM /M2K /MGC /MCL /MBT` futures + `TLT IEF SHY TIP` ETFs) so the strategy can actually generate signals. The current boot smoke is end-to-end at the cadence + heartbeat layer; signal generation requires data. The boot doesn't crash without data — `self.history(...)` returns nothing, the strategy returns empty results, and the cycle still fires + the heartbeat still POSTs. Operator can populate the volume lazily as a separate ceremony.
  - Backfill the LEAN-side `BrokerageName.INTERACTIVE_BROKERS_BROKERAGE` model call in `v1_strategy.py::initialize` — under PaperBrokerage the model is used for backtest-fee simulation only, but the IBKR-flavored model is still a valid choice and matches the fees the real api-side IBKR fills will see. Keep as-is; no change needed. (If a future LEAN release drops the IBKR model from its core assemblies the same way the brokerage was dropped, swap to `BrokerageName.DEFAULT`.)

### 2026-05-12 — Phase 1 data-seed ceremony — ETF universe seeded to `trading_lean_data`; futures deferred; first real cycle pending 21:30 UTC

- **Spec reference:** prior 2026-05-12 deploy-carryover entry (above), outstanding item #2 — "Populate the `lean_data` Docker volume with seed daily bars for the Phase 1 universe so the strategy can actually generate signals." Also `lean/README.md` file-index `lean_data` row + Troubleshooting "no historical data + no signals generated" row. Operator's session task spec: "seed the `lean_data` Docker volume on the VPS with daily bars for the Phase 1 universe so the strategy's `self.history(...)` calls return real `BarSeries` → indicators populate → `V1TrendFollowing.generate_signals(...)` produces non-empty results → `signal_emitted` events POST to the api → land in the `signals` table + audit_log."

- **Spec said:** Phase 1 universe is 7 CME micro futures (`/MES /MNQ /MYM /M2K /MGC /MCL /MBT`) + 4 NYSE bond ETFs (`TLT IEF SHY TIP`). Operator's task spec named three viable paths (QC's `lean data download` CLI; third-party CSV → LEAN-format conversion; or DataBento via LEAN CLI integration). Window: at least `MA_SLOW_DAYS=200 + ATR_LOOKBACK_DAYS=20 + 5 pad = 225` calendar days. Expected disk: ~5-15 MB compressed.

- **Three discoveries that shaped the ceremony:**

  1. **`trading_lean_data` was NOT empty** — the prior session's "empty volume" assumption was wrong. The `quantconnect/lean:latest` image bundles 233 MB of tutorial data across `alternative/`, `cfd/`, `crypto/`, `cryptofuture/`, `equity/`, `forex/`, `future/`, `futureoption/`, `index/`, `indexoption/`, `option/`, plus `market-hours/` and `symbol-properties/`. On first compose-up the volume initializes from the image's `/Lean/Data/` so the operator inherits the tutorial bundle automatically. However: **the bundle covers `/ES` + `/GC` full-size demo contracts + popular equities (SPY/AAPL/QQQ/etc.); zero coverage of the Phase 1 universe.** Specifically: `future/cme/daily/` has only `es_*.zip`; `future/comex/daily/` has only `gc_*.zip`; `future/nymex/` has only a `margins/` subdirectory (no daily data); `equity/usa/daily/` has 21 tutorial tickers (`aaa.zip aapl.zip aig.zip bac.zip bno.zip eem.zip fb.zip foxa.zip gooav.zip goocv.zip goog.zip googl.zip ibm.zip iwm.zip nwsa.zip qqq.zip spy.zip uso.zip uw.zip wm.zip wmi.zip`) and **none of `tlt/ief/shy/tip`**. So the data-seed problem reduced to "add 4 ETFs + 7 futures to a populated volume", not "build a volume from scratch."

  2. **QC's `lean data download` for futures is paid-tier.** QC's "US Futures Daily History by AlgoSeek" data package requires a paid organization tier; the public pricing page is gated behind login. The same DP-025-class tier dependency that retired the ObjectStore-poll architecture on Day 28 binds the futures-data-download path too. DataBento integration via LEAN CLI is workable but adds a third-party subscription. Costing the Phase 1 futures coverage at zero is structurally not possible via the canonical LEAN-format paths.

  3. **The strategy gracefully tolerates per-market history gaps.** `lean/v1_strategy.py::_build_bar_series` returns `None` on any history failure (empty series, parse error, exception), the caller appends to `history_failures` and skips the market, the cycle's `active_universe` shrinks but the cycle still completes. So the ETF-only seed unlocks the end-to-end pipeline verification (heartbeat 202 → cycle invocation → strategy.generate_signals → signal_emitted POST → audit row) without requiring futures coverage on day one. Futures stay at `v1_history_unavailable` per cycle, the 4 ETFs generate real signals.

- **Actual decision:** Option 1 — **seed the 4 bond ETFs only via free Yahoo Finance daily data**; defer the 7 futures to a future cost-impact decision. Cost: $0/mo. Hot-fix scope (no `risk-review-approved` label; pure data ceremony, no repo code changes; only `Docs/decisions-log.md` modified in-repo).

- **Rationale:**
  - **Cost.** The pre-authorization spec required pinging the operator before any paid subscription; ETF-only via Yahoo skips that gate entirely.
  - **Operator-burn alignment.** The operator has previously declined paid data subscriptions in the burn ledger. Adding $7-50/mo for futures data is a decision that belongs in a dedicated discussion with cost-impact framing, not buried in a seed ceremony.
  - **Pipeline-verification completeness.** All 4 phases of the post-pivot pipeline (LEAN history fetch → strategy.generate_signals → HTTP POST → api ingestion → audit chain row) are exercised by ETF-only signals. Verifying with 4 markets is sufficient to declare the end-to-end pipeline alive; the futures coverage is a universe-expansion concern, not a pipeline-correctness concern.
  - **Reversibility.** Adding 4 ETF zips to the volume is fully reversible (`docker run --rm -v trading_lean_data:/Lean/Data alpine rm /Lean/Data/equity/usa/daily/{tlt,ief,shy,tip}.zip` etc.). Audit chain growth from real signals is the one irreversible piece — operator confirmed via explicit AskUserQuestion approval before the volume copy + restart.
  - **Sub-universe alignment with strategy intent.** The Phase 1 strategy treats the bond-ETF cluster as the defensive arm of the universe. ETF-only first cycles are a coherent subset of the intended architecture (the bond ETFs ARE Phase 1; they're not a tutorial substitute).

- **What was shipped (the ceremony, in operator-readable steps):**

  1. **Sourced daily bars via `yfinance` 1.3.0** in a local Python virtualenv. Window: `start=2024-12-01` → `end=2026-05-12` (yfinance exclusive end, so last bar = 2026-05-11 finalized close — today's session is still open in real-time at the time of the ceremony, 14:48 ET). Per-ticker `yf.Ticker(t).history(...)` calls to avoid the multi-ticker SQLite cache lock that hit `TIP` on the first batched attempt. Each ticker returned **360 trading-day bars** (well above the 225-day warmup requirement). Latest closes:
     - `TLT` $85.56
     - `IEF` $94.64
     - `SHY` $82.22
     - `TIP` $111.31
  2. **Cross-checked TLT close against public reference** — Yahoo Finance + web search reported TLT range on 2026-05-12 = $84.95-$85.46 (today, still open) versus our 2026-05-11 finalized close of $85.56 (yesterday) — both internally consistent given the small overnight move.
  3. **Converted to LEAN equity-daily on-disk format.** Build script at `/tmp/lean_seed/build_seed.py` (operator-side artifact; not committed to repo). Output structure per ticker:
     - `equity/usa/daily/<lower>.zip` — single `<lower>.csv` containing `YYYYMMDD 00:00,O*10000,H*10000,L*10000,C*10000,V` rows (LEAN's scaled-by-10000 deci-cent equity-daily convention). Verified via decode round-trip: TLT 2026-05-11 = `20260511 00:00,858800,859200,854900,855600,17699500` → 855600/10000 = $85.56 ✓.
     - `equity/usa/map_files/<lower>.csv` — 2-row `<date>,<lower>,P` (NYSE Arca exchange code for bond ETFs).
     - `equity/usa/factor_files/<lower>.csv` — 2-row `<date>,1,1,1` (no dividend or split adjustments — strategy uses raw closes).
     - Per-ticker zip sizes: TLT 5759b, IEF 5306b, SHY 4496b, TIP 5226b. Total compressed staging: 22.5 KB across 12 files (4 zips + 4 map + 4 factor).
  4. **Staged on VPS** at `/tmp/lean_seed_stage/` via `tar --no-mac-metadata --no-xattrs -czf` (macOS `tar` defaults emit `._<file>` AppleDouble metadata; `COPYFILE_DISABLE=1 + --no-mac-metadata` produces a clean archive). Initial scp attempt without these flags polluted the staging dir with `._<file>` sidecars; cleaned up and re-archived.
  5. **`docker cp` into `trading_lean_data` volume** via transient `alpine` container with both the volume and the staging dir mounted: `docker run --rm -v trading_lean_data:/Lean/Data -v /tmp/lean_seed_stage:/seed:ro alpine cp -rv /seed/equity/. /Lean/Data/equity/`. The seed script is operator-side ceremony scaffolding; per the task spec it could optionally be canonicalized under `scripts/seed_lean_data.py` or `deploy/lean_local/seed.sh` (both hot-fix paths) if the operator wants a reproducible script in-repo.
